




|| namau tassa bhagavatau arahatau sammāsambuddhassa||

khuddakanikāyai


jātakapāḷi

(dutiyau bhāgau)


17. cattālīsanipātau

521. taisakuṇajātakaṃ (1)

1.

‘‘vaissantaraṃ  taṃ pucchāmi, sakuṇa bhaddamatthu tai|

raj‍jaṃ kāraitukāmaina, kiṃ su kic‍caṃ kataṃ varaṃ’’||

2.

‘‘cirassaṃ vata maṃ tātau, kaṃsau bārāṇasiggahau|

pamattau appamattaṃ maṃ, pitā puttaṃ acaudayi||

3.

‘‘paṭhamainaiva vitathaṃ, kaudhaṃ hāsaṃ nivārayai|

tatau kic‍cāni kāraiyya, taṃ vataṃ āhu khattiya||

4.

‘‘yaṃ  tvaṃ tāta tapaukammaṃ tapai kammaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), pubbai katamasaṃsayaṃ|

rattau duṭṭhau ca yaṃ kayirā, na taṃ kayirā tatau puna punaṃ (pī॰)||

5.

‘‘khattiyassa pamattassa, raṭṭhasmiṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhana|

sabbai bhaugā vinassanti, rañ‍ñau taṃ vuc‍catai aghaṃ||

6.

‘‘sirī tāta alakkhī ca sirī ca tāta lakkhī ca (syā॰ pī॰), pucchitā aitadabravuṃ|

uṭṭhāna uṭṭhānai (syā॰) vīriyai pausai, ramāhaṃ anusūyakai||

7.

‘‘usūyakai  duhadayai, purisai kammadussakai|

kālakaṇṇī mahārāja, ramati ramāti (ka॰) cak‍kabhañ‍janī||

8.

‘‘sau tvaṃ sabbaisu suhadayau sau tvaṃ sabbaisaṃ suhadayau (syā॰ pī॰), sau tvaṃ sabbai suhadayau (ka॰), sabbaisaṃ rakkhitau bhava|

alakkhiṃ nuda mahārāja, lakkhyā bhava nivaisanaṃ||

9.

‘‘sa  lakkhīdhitisampan‍nau, purisau hi mahaggatau|

amittānaṃ kāsipati, mūlaṃ aggañ‍ca chindati||

10.

‘‘sak‍kaupi hi bhūtapati, uṭṭhānai nappamaj‍jati|

sa kalyāṇai dhitiṃ katvā, uṭṭhānai kurutai manau||

11.

‘‘gandhabbā pitarau daivā, sājīvā sañ‍jīvā (pī॰) haunti tādinau|

uṭṭhāhatau uṭṭhahatau (syā॰ pī॰) appamaj‍jatau mappamaj‍jatau (ka॰), anutiṭṭhanti daivatā||

12.

‘‘sau appamattau ak‍kuddhau ak‍kuṭṭhau (pī॰), tāta kic‍cāni kāraya|

vāyamassu ca kic‍caisu, nālasau vindatai sukhaṃ||

13.

‘‘tatthaiva tai vattapadā, aisāva aisā ca (pī॰) anusāsanī|

alaṃ mittai sukhāpaituṃ, amittānaṃ dukhāya dukkhāya (pī॰) ca’’||

14.

‘‘sakkhisi tvaṃ sakkhī tuvaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kuṇḍalini, mañ‍ñasi khattabandhuni khattiyabandhunī (pī॰)|

raj‍jaṃ kāraitukāmaina, kiṃ su kic‍caṃ kataṃ varaṃ’’||

15.

‘‘dvaiva tāta padakāni, yattha yaisu (pī॰) sabbaṃ patiṭṭhitaṃ|

aladdhassa ca yau lābhau, laddhassa cānurakkhaṇā||

16.

‘‘amac‍cai tāta jānāhi, dhīrai atthassa kauvidai|

anakkhā kitavai tāta, asauṇḍai avināsakai||

17.

‘‘yau ca taṃ tāta rakkhaiyya, dhanaṃ yañ‍caiva tai siyā|

sūtauva rathaṃ saṅgaṇhai, sau tai kic‍cāni kārayai||

18.

‘‘susaṅgahitantajanau, sayaṃ vittaṃ avaikkhiya|

nidhiñ‍ca iṇadānañ‍ca, na karai parapattiyā||

19.

‘‘sayaṃ  āyaṃ  vayaṃ āyavayaṃ (pī॰) jañ‍ñā, sayaṃ jañ‍ñā katākataṃ|

niggaṇhai niggahārahaṃ, paggaṇhai paggahārahaṃ||

20.

‘‘sayaṃ jānapadaṃ atthaṃ, anusāsa rathaisabha|

mā tai adhammikā yuttā, dhanaṃ raṭṭhañ‍ca nāsayuṃ||

21.

‘‘mā ca vaigaina kic‍cāni, karausi kāraisi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kārayaisi vā|

vaigasā hi kataṃ kammaṃ, mandau pacchānutappati||

22.

‘‘mā tai adhisarai muñ‍ca, subāḷhamadhikaudhitaṃ kaupitaṃ (sī॰ syā॰)|

kaudhasā hi bahū phītā, kulā akulataṃ gatā||

23.

‘‘mā tāta issaraumhīti, anatthāya patārayi|

itthīnaṃ purisānañ‍ca, mā tai āsi dukhudrayau||

24.

‘‘apaitalaumahaṃsassa, rañ‍ñau kāmānusārinau|

sabbai bhaugā vinassanti, rañ‍ñau taṃ vuc‍catai aghaṃ||

25.

‘‘tatthaiva tai vattapadā, aisāva anusāsanī|

dakkhassudāni puñ‍ñakarau, asauṇḍau avināsakau|

sīlavāssu sīlavāssa (ṭīkā) mahārāja, dussīlau vinipātikau’’ vinipātakau (pī॰)||

26.

‘‘apucchimha kausiyagauttaṃ apucchimhā kausiyagauttaṃ (syā॰), apucchamhāpi kausikaṃ (pī॰), kuṇḍaliniṃ tathaiva ca|

tvaṃ dāni vadaihi jambuka jambuka tvaṃ dāni vadaihi (syā॰ pī॰), balānaṃ balamuttamaṃ’’||

27.

‘‘balaṃ pañ‍cavidhaṃ laukai, purisasmiṃ mahaggatai|

tattha bāhubalaṃ nāma, carimaṃ vuc‍catai balaṃ||

28.

‘‘bhaugabalañ‍ca dīghāvu, dutiyaṃ vuc‍catai balaṃ|

amac‍cabalañ‍ca  dīghāvu, tatiyaṃ vuc‍catai balaṃ||

29.

‘‘abhijac‍cabalaṃ caiva, taṃ catutthaṃ asaṃsayaṃ|

yāni caitāni sabbāni, adhigaṇhāti paṇḍitau||

30.

‘‘taṃ balānaṃ balaṃ saiṭṭhaṃ, aggaṃ pañ‍ñābaṃ balaṃ varaṃ (sī॰)|

pañ‍ñābalainupatthaddhau, atthaṃ vindati paṇḍitau||

31.

‘‘api  cai labhati mandau, phītaṃ dharaṇimuttamaṃ|

akāmassa pasayhaṃ vā, añ‍ñau taṃ paṭipaj‍jati||

32.

‘‘abhijātaupi cai hauti, raj‍jaṃ laddhāna khattiyau|

duppañ‍ñau hi kāsipati, sabbainapi na jīvati||

33.

‘‘pañ‍ñāva  sutaṃ vinicchinī pañ‍ñā sutavinicchinī (syā॰ pī॰), pañ‍ñā kitti silaukavaḍḍhanī vaddhanī (pī॰)|

pañ‍ñāsahitau narau idha, api dukkhai sukhāni vindati||

34.

‘‘pañ‍ñañ‍ca khau asussūsaṃ, na kauci adhigacchati|

bahussutaṃ anāgamma, dhammaṭṭhaṃ avinibbhujaṃ||

35.

‘‘yau ca dhammavibhaṅgañ‍ñū yau dhammañ‍ca vibhāgañ‍ñū (pī॰), kāluṭṭhāyī matanditau|

anuṭṭhahati kālaina, kammaphalaṃ tassa ijjhati kammaphalaṃ tassijjhati, phalaṃ tassa samijjhati (ka॰)||

36.

‘‘anāyatana nā’nāyatana (pī॰) sīlassa, anāyatana nā’nāyatana (pī॰) saivinau|

na nibbindiyakārissa, sammadatthau vipac‍cati||

37.

‘‘ajjhattañ‍ca payuttassa, tathāyatanasaivinau|

anibbindiyakārissa, sammadatthau vipac‍cati||

38.

‘‘yaugappayaugasaṅkhātaṃ, sambhatassānurakkhaṇaṃ|

tāni  tvaṃ tāta saivassu, mā akammāya randhayi|

akammunā hi dummaidhau, naḷāgāraṃva sīdati’’||

39.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, mātāpitūsu khattiya|

idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, rāja saggaṃ gamissasi||

40.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, puttadāraisu khattiya|

idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, rāja saggaṃ gamissasi||

41.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, mittāmac‍caisu khattiya|

idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, rāja saggaṃ gamissasi||

42.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, vāhanaisu balaisu ca|

idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, rāja saggaṃ gamissasi||

43.

‘‘dhammaṃ  cara mahārāja, gāmaisu nigamaisu ca…pai॰…||

44.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, raṭṭhaisu raṭṭhai (pī॰) janapadaisu ca…pai॰…||

45.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, samaṇa samaṇai (syā॰ ka॰) brāhmaṇaisu ca…pai॰…||

46.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, migapakkhīsu khattiya|

idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, rāja saggaṃ gamissasi||

47.

‘‘dhammaṃ  cara mahārāja, dhammau ciṇṇau sukhāvahau dhammau suciṇṇau sukhamāvahati (ka॰)|

idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, rāja saggaṃ gamissasi||

48.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, saindā indau (pī॰), sindā (ka॰) daivā sabrahmakā|

suciṇṇaina divaṃ pattā, mā dhammaṃ rāja pāmadau pamādau (pī॰ ka॰)||

49.

‘‘tatthaiva  tai vaitai (pī॰) vattapadā, aisāva aisā ca (pī॰) anusāsanī|

sappañ‍ñasaivī kalyāṇī, samattaṃ sāma sāmaṃ (ka॰) taṃ vidū’’ti||

taisakuṇajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

522. sarabhaṅgajātakaṃ (2)

50.

‘‘alaṅkatā kuṇḍalinau suvatthā, vaiḷuriyamuttātharukhaggabandhā baddhā (pī॰)|

rathaisabhā tiṭṭhatha kai nu tumhai, kathaṃ vau jānanti manussalaukai’’||

51.

‘‘ahamaṭṭhakau bhīmarathau panāyaṃ, kāliṅgarājā pana uggatauyaṃ uggatau ayaṃ (pī॰), uggatāyaṃ (ka॰)|

susañ‍ñatānaṃ isīnaṃ susañ‍ñatānisinaṃ (pī॰) dassanāya, idhāgatā pucchitāyaimha pañhai’’||

52.

‘‘vaihāyasaṃ  tiṭṭhasi tiṭṭhati (pī॰) antalikkhai, pathaddhunau pan‍narasaiva candau|

pucchāmi taṃ yakkha mahānubhāva, kathaṃ taṃ jānanti manussalaukai’’||

53.

‘‘yamāhu daivaisu sujampatīti, maghavāti  taṃ āhu manussalaukai|

sa daivarājā idamaj‍ja pattau, susañ‍ñatānaṃ isīnaṃ dassanāya’’||

54.

‘‘dūrai sutā nau isayau samāgatā, mahiddhikā iddhiguṇūpapan‍nā|

vandāmi tai ayirai pasan‍nacittau, yai jīvalaukaittha manussasaiṭṭhā’’||

55.

gandhau isīnaṃ ciradikkhitānaṃ dakkhitānaṃ (syā॰ pī॰), kāyā cutau gacchati mālutaina|

itau paṭik‍kamma sahassanaitta, gandhau isīnaṃ asuci daivarāja’’||

56.

‘‘gandhau isīnaṃ ciradikkhitānaṃ, kāyā cutau gacchatu mālutaina|

vicitrapupphaṃ surabhiṃva mālaṃ, gandhañ‍ca aitaṃ pāṭikaṅkhāma bhantai|

na haittha daivā paṭik‍kūlasañ‍ñinau’’||

57.

‘‘purindadau bhūtapatī yasassī, daivānamindau sak‍kau idaṃ padaṃ natthi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ pautthakaisu) maghavā sujampati|

sa daivarājā asuragaṇappamaddanau, aukāsamākaṅkhati pañha pucchituṃ||

58.

‘‘kau  naivimaisaṃ idha paṇḍitānaṃ, pañhai puṭṭhau nipuṇai byākarissati|

tiṇṇañ‍ca rañ‍ñaṃ manujādhipānaṃ, daivānamindassa ca vāsavassa’’||

59.

‘‘ayaṃ  isi isī (sī॰ pī॰) sarabhaṅgau tapassī yasassī (sī॰), yatau jātau viratau maithunasmā|

ācairaputtau ācariyaputtau (pī॰ ka॰) suvinītarūpau, sau naisaṃ pañhāni viyākarissati’’||

60.

‘‘kauṇḍañ‍ña pañhāni viyākarauhi, yācanti taṃ isayau sādhurūpā|

kauṇḍañ‍ña aisau manujaisu dhammau, yaṃ vuddha vaddha (pī॰), buddha (ka॰) māgacchati aisa bhārau’’||

61.

‘‘katāvakāsā  pucchantu bhauntau, yaṃ kiñ‍ci pañhaṃ manasābhipatthitaṃ|

ahañhi taṃ taṃ vau viyākarissaṃ, ñatvā sayaṃ laukamimaṃ parañ‍ca’’||

62.

‘‘tatau ca maghavā sak‍kau, atthadassī purindadau|

apucchi paṭhamaṃ pañhaṃ, yañ‍cāsi abhipatthitaṃ’’||

63.

‘‘kiṃ sū vadhitvā na kadāci saucati, kissappahānaṃ isayau vaṇṇayanti|

kassīdha  vuttaṃ pharusaṃ khamaitha, akkhāhi mai kauṇḍañ‍ña aitamatthaṃ’’||

64.

‘‘kaudhaṃ vadhitvā na kadāci saucati, makkhappahānaṃ isayau vaṇṇayanti|

sabbaisaṃ vuttaṃ pharusaṃ khamaitha, aitaṃ khantiṃ uttamamāhu santau’’||

65.

‘‘sak‍kā ubhin‍naṃ hi dvin‍naṃ (pī॰) vacanaṃ titikkhituṃ, sadisassa vā saiṭṭhatarassa saiṭṭhanarassa (pī॰) vāpi|

kathaṃ nu hīnassa vacau khamaitha, akkhāhi mai kauṇḍañ‍ña aitamatthaṃ’’||

66.

‘‘bhayā  hi saiṭṭhassa vacau khamaitha, sārambhahaitū pana sādisassa|

yau cīdha hīnassa vacau khamaitha, aitaṃ khantiṃ uttamamāhu santau’’||

67.

‘‘kathaṃ vijañ‍ñā catupattharūpaṃ catumaṭṭharūpaṃ (syā॰ pī॰), saiṭṭhaṃ sarikkhaṃ athavāpi hīnaṃ|

virūparūpaina caranti santau, tasmā hi sabbaisaṃ vacau khamaitha’’||

68.

‘‘na haitamatthaṃ mahatīpi sainā, sarājikā yujjhamānā labhaitha|

yaṃ  khantimā sappurisau labhaitha, khantī balassūpasamanti vairā’’||

69.

‘‘subhāsitaṃ tai anumaudiyāna, añ‍ñaṃ taṃ pucchāmi tadiṅgha brūhi|

yathā ahuṃ ahū (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) daṇḍakī nāḷikairau nāḷikīrau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), athaj‍junau kalābu cāpi rājā|

taisaṃ gatiṃ brūhi supāpakamminaṃ, katthūpapan‍nā isinaṃ vihaiṭhakā’’||

70.

‘‘kisañhi kisaṃpi (pī॰) vacchaṃ avakiriya daṇḍakī, ucchin‍namūlau sajanau saraṭṭhau|

kuk‍kuḷanāmai nirayamhi pac‍cati, tassa phuliṅgāni patanti kāyai||

71.

‘‘yau sañ‍ñatai pabbajitai ahaiṭhayi avañ‍casi (pī॰), dhammaṃ bhaṇantai samaṇai adūsakai|

taṃ nāḷikairaṃ sunakhā parattha, saṅgamma khādanti viphandamānaṃ||

72.

‘‘athaj‍junau  nirayai sattisūlai, avaṃsirau patitau uddhaṃpādau uddhapādau (syā॰), addhapādau (pī॰)|

aṅgīrasaṃ gautamaṃ haiṭhayitvā, khantiṃ tapassiṃ cirabrahmacāriṃ||

73.

‘‘yau  khaṇḍasau pabbajitaṃ achaidayi, khantiṃ vadantaṃ samaṇaṃ adūsakaṃ|

kalābuvīciṃ upapaj‍ja pac‍cati, mahāpatāpaṃ mahābhitāpaṃ (pī॰) kaṭukaṃ bhayānakaṃ||

74.

‘‘aitāni sutvā nirayāni paṇḍitau, añ‍ñāni pāpiṭṭhatarāni caittha|

dhammaṃ carai samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu, aivaṅkarau saggamupaiti ṭhānaṃ’’||

75.

‘‘subhāsitaṃ  tai anumaudiyāna, añ‍ñaṃ taṃ pucchāmi tadiṅgha brūhi|

kathaṃvidhaṃ sīlavantaṃ vadanti, kathaṃvidhaṃ pañ‍ñavantaṃ vadanti|

kathaṃvidhaṃ sappurisaṃ vadanti, kathaṃvidhaṃ nau siri nau jahāti’’||

76.

‘‘kāyaina vācāya ca yau’dha yau ca (pī॰) sañ‍ñatau, manasā ca kiñ‍ci na karauti pāpaṃ|

na attahaitū alikaṃ bhaṇaiti bhaṇāti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), tathāvidhaṃ sīlavantaṃ vadanti||

77.

‘‘gambhīrapañhaṃ manasābhicintayaṃ manasā vicintayaṃ (sī॰), nāc‍cāhitaṃ kamma karauti luddaṃ|

kālāgataṃ  kālābhataṃ (pī॰) atthapadaṃ na riñ‍cati, tathāvidhaṃ pañ‍ñavantaṃ vadanti||

78.

‘‘yau  vai katañ‍ñū katavaidi dhīrau, kalyāṇamittau daḷhabhatti ca hauti|

dukhitassa sak‍kac‍ca karauti kic‍caṃ, tathāvidhaṃ sappurisaṃ vadanti||

79.

‘‘aitaihi sabbaihi guṇaihupaitau, saddhau mudū saṃvibhāgī vadañ‍ñū|

saṅgāhakaṃ sakhilaṃ saṇhavācaṃ, tathāvidhaṃ nau siri nau jahāti’’||

80.

‘‘subhāsitaṃ tai anumaudiyāna, añ‍ñaṃ taṃ pucchāmi tadiṅgha brūhi|

sīlaṃ siriñ‍cāpi satañ‍ca dhammaṃ, pañ‍ñañ‍ca kaṃ saiṭṭhataraṃ vadanti’’||

81.

‘‘pañ‍ñā hi saiṭṭhā kusalā vadanti, nakkhattarājāriva tārakānaṃ|

sīlaṃ sīrī cāpi satañ‍ca dhammau dhammā (pī॰), anvāyikā pañ‍ñavatau bhavanti’’||

82.

‘‘subhāsitaṃ tai anumaudiyāna, añ‍ñaṃ taṃ pucchāmi tadiṅgha brūhi|

kathaṃkarau  kintikarau kimācaraṃ, kiṃ saivamānau labhatīdha pañ‍ñaṃ|

pañ‍ñāya dānippaṭipaṃ dāni paṭipadaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) vadaihi, kathaṃkarau pañ‍ñavā hauti mac‍cau’’||

83.

‘‘saivaitha vuddhai nipuṇai bahussutai, uggāhakau ca paripucchakau siyā|

suṇaiyya sak‍kac‍ca subhāsitāni, aivaṃkarau pañ‍ñavā hauti mac‍cau||

84.

‘‘ pañ‍ñavā kāmaguṇai avaikkhati, anic‍catau dukkhatau raugatau ca|

aivaṃ vipassī pajahāti chandaṃ, dukkhaisu kāmaisu mahabbhayaisu||

85.

‘‘sa vītarāgau pavinaiyya dausaṃ, maittaṃ maitta (syā॰ ka॰) cittaṃ bhāvayai bhāvaiyya (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) appamāṇaṃ|

sabbaisu bhūtaisu nidhāya daṇḍaṃ, aninditau brahmamupaiti ṭhānaṃ’’||

86.

‘‘mahatthiyaṃ mahiddhiyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) āgamanaṃ ahausi, tavamaṭṭhakā maṭṭhaka (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) bhīmarathassa cāpi|

kāliṅgarājassa ca uggatassa, sabbaisa  vau kāmarāgau pahīnau’’||

87.

‘‘aivamaitaṃ paracittavaidi, sabbaisa nau kāmarāgau pahīnau|

karauhi aukāsamanuggahāya, yathā gatiṃ tai abhisambhavaima’’||

88.

‘‘karaumi aukāsamanuggahāya, tathā hi vau kāmarāgau pahīnau|

pharātha kāyaṃ vipulāya pītiyā, yathā gatiṃ mai abhisambhavaitha’’||

89.

‘‘sabbaṃ  karissāma tavānusāsaniṃ, yaṃ yaṃ tuvaṃ vakkhasi bhūripañ‍ña|

pharāma kāyaṃ vipulāya pītiyā, yathā gatiṃ tai abhisambhavaima’’||

90.

‘‘katāya katāyaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) vacchassa kisassa pūjā, gacchantu bhauntau isayau sādhurūpā|

jhānai ratā hautha sadā samāhitā, aisā ratī pabbajitassa saiṭṭhā’’||

91.

‘‘sutvāna  gāthā paramatthasaṃhitā, subhāsitā isinā paṇḍitaina|

tai vaidajātā anumaudamānā, pak‍kāmu  pak‍kamu (ka॰) daivā daivapuraṃ yasassinau||

92.

‘‘gāthā imā atthavatī subyañ‍janā, subhāsitā isinā paṇḍitaina|

yau kaucimā aṭṭhikatvā aṭṭhiṃ katvā (ka॰) suṇaiyya, labhaitha pubbāpariyaṃ visaisaṃ|

laddhāna pubbāpariyaṃ visaisaṃ, adassanaṃ mac‍curājassa gacchai’’||

93.

‘‘sālissarau sāriputtau, maiṇḍissarau ca kassapau|

pabbatau anuruddhau ca, kac‍cāyanau ca daivalau daivilau (syā॰ ka॰)||

94.

‘‘anusissau ca ānandau, kisavacchau ca kaulitau|

nāradau udāyī thairau nāradau puṇṇau mantānīputtau (sī॰), parisā buddhaparisā|

sarabhaṅgau laukanāthau, aivaṃ dhāraitha jātaka’’nti||

sarabhaṅgajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ|

523. alambusājātakaṃ (3)

95.

‘‘atha bravi brahā indau, vatrabhū jayataṃ pitā|

daivakañ‍ñaṃ parābhaitvā, sudhammāyaṃ alambusaṃ||

96.

‘‘missai daivā taṃ yācanti, tāvatiṃsā saindakā|

isippalaubhanai  isipalaubhikai (sī॰ syā॰), isiṃ palaubhikai (pī॰) gaccha, isisiṅgaṃ alambusai||

97.

‘‘purāyaṃ amhai ac‍caiti nāc‍caiti (syā॰ ka॰), vattavā vatavā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) brahmacariyavā|

nibbānābhiratau vuddhau vaddhau (pī॰), buddhau (syā॰ ka॰), tassa maggāni āvara’’||

98.

‘‘daivarāja kimaiva tvaṃ, mamaiva tuvaṃ sikkhasi|

isippalaubhanai isipalaubhikai (sī॰ syā॰), isiṃ palaubhikai (pī॰) gaccha, santi añ‍ñāpi accharā||

99.

‘‘mādisiyau  pavarā caiva, asaukai nandanai vanai|

tāsampi hautu pariyāyau, tāpi yantu palaubhanā’’ palaubhikā (syā॰ pī॰)||

100.

‘‘addhā  hi sac‍caṃ bhaṇasi, santi añ‍ñāpi accharā|

tādisiyau pavarā caiva, asaukai nandanai vanai||

101.

‘‘na tā aivaṃ pajānanti, pāricariyaṃ pumaṃ gatā|

yādisaṃ tvaṃ pajānāsi, nāri sabbaṅgasaubhanai||

102.

‘‘tvamaiva gaccha kalyāṇi, itthīnaṃ pavarā casi|

tavaiva vaṇṇarūpaina, savasamānayissasi’’ vasamānāpayissasi (syā॰), vasamānāmayissasi (pī॰), taṃ vasamānayissasi (ka॰)||

103.

‘‘na vāhaṃ na gamissāmi, daivarājaina paisitā|

vibhaimi caitaṃ āsāduṃ, uggataijau hi brāhmaṇau||

104.

‘‘anaikai nirayaṃ pattā, isimāsādiyā janā|

āpan‍nā mauhasaṃsāraṃ, tasmā laumāni haṃsayai’’||

105.

‘‘idaṃ vatvāna pak‍kāmi, accharā kāmavaṇṇinī|

missā missitu missaitu (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) micchantī, isisiṅgaṃ alambusā||

106.

‘‘sā ca taṃ vanamaugayha, isisiṅgaina rakkhitaṃ|

bimbajālakasañchan‍naṃ, samantā aḍḍhayaujanaṃ||

107.

‘‘pātauva pātarāsamhi, udaṇhasamayaṃ udayasamayaṃ (syā॰), udantasamayaṃ (ka॰) pati|

aggiṭṭhaṃ parimaj‍jantaṃ, isisiṅgaṃ upāgami’’||

108.

‘‘kā nu vij‍jurivābhāsi, ausadhī viya tārakā|

vicittahatthābharaṇā vicittavatthābharaṇā (sī॰), āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā āmuk‍kamaṇikuṇḍalā (?)||

109.

‘‘ādic‍cavaṇṇasaṅkāsā, haimacandanagandhinī|

sañ‍ñatūrū mahāmāyā, kumārī cārudassanā||

110.

‘‘vilaggā vilākā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) mudukā suddhā, pādā tai suppatiṭṭhitā|

gamanā kāmanīyā kamanā kamanīyā (sī॰ pī॰) tai, harantiyaiva mai manau||

111.

‘‘anupubbāva  tai ūrū, nāganāsasamūpamā|

vimaṭṭhā tuyhaṃ sussauṇī, akkhassa phalakaṃ yathā||

112.

‘‘uppalassaiva kiñ‍jakkhā, nābhi tai sādhu saṇṭhitā|

pūrā kaṇhañ‍janassaiva, dūratau paṭidissati||

113.

‘‘duvidhā jātā urajā, avaṇṭā sādhu pac‍cudā|

payaudharā apatitā appatītā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), aḍḍhalābusamā thanā||

114.

‘‘dīghā kambutalābhāsā, gīvā aiṇaiyyakā yathā|

paṇḍarāvaraṇā vaggu, catutthamanasan‍nibhā||

115.

‘‘uddhaggā  ca adhaggā ca, dumaggaparimaj‍jitā|

duvijā nailasambhūtā, dantā tava sudassanā||

116.

‘‘apaṇḍarā lauhitantā, jiñ‍jūka jiñ‍juka (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) phalasan‍nibhā|

āyatā  ca visālā ca, naittā tava sudassanā||

117.

‘‘nātidīghā susammaṭṭhā, kanakabyā kanakaggā (pī॰) samaucitā|

uttamaṅgaruhā tuyhaṃ, kaisā candanagandhikā||

118.

‘‘yāvatā kasigaurakkhā, vāṇijānaṃ vaṇijānaṃ (pī॰) ca yā gati|

isīnañ‍ca parak‍kantaṃ, sañ‍ñatānaṃ tapassinaṃ||

119.

‘‘na tai samasamaṃ passai, asmiṃ pathavi puthuvi (pī॰) maṇḍalai|

kau vā tvaṃ kassa vā puttau, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ mayaṃ’’||

120.

‘‘na pañhakālau bhaddantai, kassapaivaṃ gatai sati|

aihi samma ramissāma, ubhau asmākamassamai|

aihi taṃ upagūhissaṃ upaguyhissaṃ (syā॰), ratīnaṃ kusalau bhava’’||

121.

‘‘idaṃ vatvāna pak‍kāmi, accharā kāmavaṇṇinī|

missā missitumicchantī, isisiṅgaṃ alambusā’’||

122.

‘‘sau ca vaigaina nikkhamma, chaitvā dandhaparak‍kamaṃ dandhapadak‍kamaṃ (ka॰)|

tamuttamāsu vaiṇīsu, ajjhappattau ajjhāpattau (pī॰) parāmasi|

123.

‘‘tamudāvatta  kalyāṇī, palissaji susaubhanā susaubhaṇī (syā॰ ka॰)|

cavitamhi cavi tamhā (syā॰ ka॰) brahmacariyā, yathā taṃ atha tausitā||

124.

‘‘manasā agamā indaṃ, vasantaṃ nandanai vanai|

tassā saṅkappamañ‍ñāya, maghavā daivakuñ‍jarau||

125.

‘‘pal‍laṅkaṃ pahiṇī khippaṃ, sauvaṇṇaṃ saupavāhanaṃ|

sauttaracchadapañ‍ñāsaṃ, sahassapaṭiyatthataṃ paṭikatthataṃ (sī॰)||

126.

‘‘tamainaṃ  tattha dhāraisi, urai katvāna saubhanā|

yathā aikamuhuttaṃva, tīṇi vassāni dhārayi||

127.

‘‘vimadau tīhi vassaihi, pabujjhitvāna brāhmaṇau|

addasāsi harita harī (pī॰) rukkhai, samantā aggiyāyanaṃ||

128.

‘‘navapattavanaṃ phul‍laṃ, kaukilaggaṇaghausitaṃ|

samantā pavilaukaitvā, rudaṃ assūni vattayi||

129.

‘‘na  juhai na japai jappai (ka॰) mantai, aggihuttaṃ pahāpitaṃ|

kau nu mai pāricariyāya, pubbai cittaṃ palaubhayi||

130.

‘‘arañ‍ñai mai viharatau, yau mai taijā ha sambhutaṃ sambhataṃ (pī॰)|

nānāratnaparipūraṃ, nāvaṃva gaṇhi aṇṇavai’’||

131.

‘‘ahaṃ tai pāricariyāya, daivarājaina paisitā|

avadhiṃ avadhī (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) cittaṃ cittaina, pamādau pamādā (syā॰ pī॰) tvaṃ na bujjhasi’’||

132.

‘‘imāni kira maṃ tātau, kassapau anusāsati|

kamalāsadisitthiyau sarisitthiyau (syā॰ pī॰), tāyau bujjhaisi māṇava||

133.

‘‘urai gaṇḍāyau bujjhaisi, tāyau bujjhaisi māṇava|

ic‍cānusāsi maṃ tātau, yathā maṃ anukampakau||

134.

‘‘tassāhaṃ vacanaṃ nākaṃ, pitu vuddhassa sāsanaṃ|

arañ‍ñai nimmanussamhi, svaj‍ja jhāyāmi svāj‍jajjhāyāmi (sī॰ pī॰) aikakau||

135.

‘‘sauhaṃ  tathā karissāmi, dhiratthu jīvitaina mai|

puna vā tādisau haissaṃ, maraṇaṃ mai bhavissati’’||

136.

‘‘tassa  taijaṃ taijañ‍ca (sī॰ pī॰) vīriyañ‍ca, dhitiṃ dhitiñ‍ca (pī॰) ñatvā avaṭṭhitaṃ suvaḍḍhitaṃ (sī॰)|

sirasā aggahī pādai, isisiṅgaṃ alambusā||

137.

‘‘mā mai kujjha kujjhi (pī॰) mahāvīra, mā mai kujjha kujjhi (pī॰) mahāisai|

mahā atthau mayā ciṇṇau, tidasānaṃ yasassinaṃ|

tayā saṃkampitaṃ āsi, sabbaṃ daivapuraṃ tadā’’||

138.

‘‘tāvatiṃsā ca yai daivā, tidasānañ‍ca vāsavau|

tvañ‍ca bhaddai sukhī hauhi, gaccha kañ‍ñai yathāsukhaṃ’’||

139.

‘‘tassa pādai gahaitvāna, katvā ca naṃ padakkhiṇaṃ|

añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvāna, tamhā ṭhānā apak‍kami||

140.

‘‘yau ca tassāsi pal‍laṅkau, sauvaṇṇau saupavāhanau|

sauttaracchadapañ‍ñāsau, sahassapaṭiyatthatau|

tamaiva pal‍laṅkamāruyha, agā daivāna santikai||

141.

‘‘tamauk‍kamiva āyantiṃ, jalantiṃ vij‍jutaṃ yathā|

patītau sumanau vittau, daivindau adadā varaṃ’’||

142.

‘‘varañ‍cai  mai adau sak‍ka, sabbabhūtānamissara|

nisippalaubhikā na isipalaubhikā (syā॰), na isipalaubhiyaṃ (pī॰) gacchai, aitaṃ sak‍ka varaṃ varai’’ti||

alambusājātakaṃ tatiyaṃ|

524. saṅkhapālajātakaṃ (4)

143.

‘‘ariyāvakāsausi pasan‍nanaittau, mañ‍ñai  bhavaṃ pabbajitau kulamhā|

kathaṃ nu vittāni pahāya bhaugai, pabbaji nikkhamma gharā sapañ‍ña’’ sapañ‍ñau (syā॰), sapañ‍ñā (pī॰)||

144.

‘‘sayaṃ  vimānaṃ naradaiva disvā, mahānubhāvassa mahauragassa|

disvāna puñ‍ñāna mahāvipākaṃ, saddhāyahaṃ pabbajitaumhi rāja’’||

145.

‘‘na kāmakāmā na bhayā na dausā, vācaṃ musā pabbajitā bhaṇanti|

akkhāhi mai pucchitau aitamatthaṃ, sutvāna mai jāyihitippasādau’’||

146.

‘‘vāṇij‍ja vaṇij‍ja (pī॰) raṭṭhādhipa gacchamānau, pathai addasāsimhi bhaujaputtai milācaputtai (sī॰ pī॰)|

pavaddhakāyaṃ uragaṃ mahantaṃ, ādāya gacchantai pamaudamānai’’||

147.

‘‘sauhaṃ samāgamma janinda taihi, pahaṭṭhalaumau avacamhi bhītau|

kuhiṃ ayaṃ nīyati niyyati (ka॰) bhīmakāyau, nāgaina kiṃ kāhatha bhaujaputtā||

148.

‘‘nāgau ayaṃ nīyati bhaujanatthā bhaujanatthaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), pavaddhakāyau  uragau mahantau|

sāduñ‍ca thūlañ‍ca muduñ‍ca maṃsaṃ, na tvaṃ rasañ‍ñāsi vidaihaputta||

149.

‘‘itau mayaṃ gantvā sakaṃ nikaitaṃ nikaitanaṃ (pī॰), ādāya satthāni vikaupayitvā|

maṃsāni bhaukkhāma bhakkhāma (syā॰) pamaudamānā, mayañhi vai sattavau pan‍nagānaṃ||

150.

‘‘sacai ayaṃ nīyati bhaujanatthā, pavaddhakāyau uragau mahantau|

dadāmi vau balibaddāni balivaddāni (pī॰) sauḷasa, nāgaṃ imaṃ muñ‍catha bandhanasmā||

151.

‘‘addhā  hi nau bhakkhau ayaṃ manāpau, bahū ca nau uragā bhuttapubbā bahuṃ ca nau uragau bhuttapubbau (ka॰)|

karauma tai taṃ vacanaṃ aḷāra āḷāra (ka॰) aivamuparipi, mittañ‍ca nau hauhi vidaihaputta||

152.

‘‘tadassu tai bandhanā maucayiṃsu, yaṃ natthutau paṭimauk‍kassa pāsai|

muttau ca sau bandhanā nāgarājā, pak‍kāmi pācīnamukhau muhuttaṃ||

153.

‘‘gantvāna pācīnamukhau muhuttaṃ, puṇṇaihi  naittaihi palaukayī maṃ|

tadāssahaṃ piṭṭhitau anvagacchiṃ, dasaṅguliṃ añ‍jaliṃ paggahaitvā||

154.

‘‘gacchaiva  khau tvaṃ taramānarūpau, mā taṃ amittā punaraggahaisuṃ|

dukkhau hi luddaihi punā samāgamau, adassanaṃ bhaujaputtāna gaccha||

155.

‘‘agamāsi sau rahadaṃ vippasan‍naṃ, nīlaubhāsaṃ ramaṇīyaṃ sutitthaṃ|

samautataṃ samaunataṃ (syā॰ ka॰) jambuhi vaitasāhi, pāvaikkhi nittiṇṇabhayau patītau||

156.

‘‘sau taṃ pavissa na cirassa nāgau, dibbaina mai pāturahuṃ janinda|

upaṭṭhahī maṃ pitaraṃva puttau, hadayaṅgamaṃ kaṇṇasukhaṃ bhaṇantau||

157.

‘‘tvaṃ  maisi mātā ca pitā pitā ca (pī॰) aḷāra, abbhantarau pāṇadadau sahāyau|

sakañ‍ca iddhiṃ paṭilābhakausmi paṭilābhitausmi (pī॰), aḷāra passa mai nivaisanāni|

pahūtabhakkhaṃ bahuan‍napānaṃ, masak‍kasāraṃ  viya vāsavassa’’||

158.

‘‘taṃ bhūmibhāgaihi upaitarūpaṃ, asakkharā caiva mudū subhā ca|

nīcattiṇā nīcā tiṇā (syā॰ pī॰) apparajā ca bhūmi, pāsādikā yattha jahanti saukaṃ||

159.

‘‘anāvakulā vaiḷuriyūpanīlā, catuddisaṃ ambavanaṃ surammaṃ|

pak‍kā ca paisī ca phalā suphul‍lā, nic‍cautukā dhārayantī phalāni||

160.

‘‘taisaṃ vanānaṃ naradaiva majjhai, nivaisanaṃ bhassarasan‍nikāsaṃ|

rajataggaḷaṃ sauvaṇṇamayaṃ uḷāraṃ, aubhāsatī vij‍jurivantalikkhai||

161.

‘‘maṇīmayā sauṇṇamayā sauvaṇṇamayā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) uḷārā, anaikacittā satataṃ sunimmitā|

paripūrā kañ‍ñāhi alaṅkatābhi, suvaṇṇakāyūradharāhi rāja||

162.

‘‘sau saṅkhapālau taramānarūpau, pāsādamāruyha anaumavaṇṇau|

sahassathambhaṃ atulānubhāvaṃ, yatthassa  bhariyā mahaisī ahausi||

163.

‘‘aikā  ca nārī taramānarūpā, ādāya vaiḷuriyamayaṃ mahagghaṃ|

subhaṃ maṇiṃ jātimantūpapan‍naṃ, acauditā āsanamabbhihāsi||

164.

‘‘tatau maṃ uragau hatthai gahaitvā, nisīdayī pāmukhaāsanasmiṃ|

idamāsanaṃ atra bhavaṃ nisīdatu, bhavañhi mai añ‍ñatarau garūnaṃ||

165.

‘‘añ‍ñā ca nārī taramānarūpā, ādāya vāriṃ upasaṅkamitvā|

pādāni pakkhālayī mai janinda, bhariyāva bhariyā ca (pī॰) bhattū patinau piyassa||

166.

‘‘aparā ca nārī taramānarūpā, paggayha sauvaṇṇamayāya sauvaṇṇamayā (pī॰) pātiyā|

anaikasūpaṃ vividhaṃ viyañ‍janaṃ, upanāmayī bhatta manuñ‍ñarūpaṃ||

167.

‘‘turiyaihi tūriyaihi (ka॰) maṃ bhārata bhuttavantaṃ, upaṭṭhahuṃ bhattu manau viditvā|

tatuttariṃ taduttariṃ (ka॰) maṃ nipatī mahantaṃ, dibbaihi  kāmaihi anappakaihi||

168.

‘‘bhariyā  mamaitā tisatā aḷāra, sabbattamajjhā padumuttarābhā|

aḷāra aitāssu tai kāmakārā, dadāmi tai tā paricārayassu||

169.

‘‘saṃvaccharaṃ dibbarasānubhutvā, tadāssuhaṃ tadassahaṃ (pī॰) uttarimajjhabhāsiṃ uttari pac‍cabhāsiṃ (sī॰ syā॰), uttariṃ pac‍cabhāsiṃ (pī॰)|

nāgassidaṃ kinti kathañ‍ca laddhaṃ, kathajjhagamāsi vimānasaiṭṭhaṃ’’||

170.

‘‘adhic‍ca  laddhaṃ pariṇāmajaṃ tai, sayaṃkataṃ udāhu daivaihi din‍naṃ|

pucchāmi taṃ tai (pī॰) nāgarājaitamatthaṃ, kathajjhagamāsi vimānasaiṭṭhaṃ’’||

171.

‘‘nādhic‍ca laddhaṃ na pariṇāmajaṃ mai, na sayaṃkataṃ nāpi daivaihi din‍naṃ|

sakaihi kammaihi apāpakaihi, puñ‍ñaihi mai laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ’’||

172.

‘‘kiṃ tai vataṃ kiṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ, kissa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipākau|

akkhāhi mai nāgarājaitamatthaṃ, kathaṃ  nu tai laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ’’||

173.

‘‘rājā ahausiṃ magadhānamissarau, duyyaudhanau nāma mahānubhāvau|

sau ittaraṃ jīvitaṃ saṃviditvā, asassataṃ vipariṇāmadhammaṃ||

174.

‘‘an‍nañ‍ca pānañ‍ca pasan‍nacittau, sak‍kac‍ca dānaṃ vipulaṃ adāsiṃ adāsi (pī॰)|

aupānabhūtaṃ mai gharaṃ tadāsi, santappitā samaṇabrāhmaṇā ca||

175.

ayaṃ gāthā pī॰ pautthakai natthi ‘‘mālañ‍ca gandhañ‍ca vilaipanañ‍ca, padīpiyaṃ padīpayaṃ (syā॰ ka॰) yānamupassayañ‍ca|

acchādanaṃ saiyyamathan‍napānaṃ, sak‍kac‍ca dānāni adamha tattha ayaṃ gāthā pī॰ pautthakai natthi||

176.

‘‘taṃ  mai vataṃ taṃ pana brahmacariyaṃ, tassa suciṇṇassa ayaṃ vipākau|

tainaiva mai laddhamidaṃ vimānaṃ, pahūtabhakkhaṃ bahuan‍napānaṃ’’|

‘‘nac‍caihi gītaihi cupaitarūpaṃ, ciraṭṭhitikaṃ na ca sassatāyaṃ||

177.

‘‘appānubhāvā taṃ mahānubhāvaṃ, taijassinaṃ  hanti ataijavantau|

kimaiva dāṭhāvudha kiṃ paṭic‍ca, hatthatta hatthattha (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) māgacchi vanibbakānaṃ vaṇibbakānaṃ (sī॰)||

178.

‘‘bhayaṃ nu tai anvagataṃ mahantaṃ, taijau nu tai nānvagaṃ dantamūlaṃ|

kimaiva dāṭhāvudha kiṃ paṭic‍ca, kilaisamāpaj‍ji vanibbakānaṃ’’||

179.

‘‘na mai bhayaṃ anvagataṃ mahantaṃ, taijau na sak‍kā mama taihi hantuṃ taibhihantuṃ (syā॰ ka॰)|

satañ‍ca dhammāni sukittitāni, samuddavailāva durac‍cayāni||

180.

‘‘cātuddasiṃ pañ‍cadasiṃ aḷāra, upausathaṃ nic‍camupāvasāmi|

athāgamuṃ sauḷasa bhaujaputtā, raj‍juṃ gahaitvāna daḷhañ‍ca pāsaṃ||

181.

‘‘bhaitvāna nāsaṃ atikassa antakassa (ka॰) raj‍juṃ, nayiṃsu maṃ samparigayha luddā|

aitādisaṃ dukkhamahaṃ titikkhaṃ titikkhiṃ (pī॰), upausathaṃ appaṭikaupayantau’’||

182.

‘‘aikāyanai  taṃ pathai addasaṃsu, balaina  vaṇṇaina cupaitarūpaṃ|

siriyā pañ‍ñāya ca bhāvitausi, kiṃ patthayaṃ kimatthiyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) nāga tapau karausi||

183.

‘‘na puttahaitū na dhanassa haitu, na āyunau cāpi aḷāra haitu|

manussayauniṃ abhipatthayānau, tasmā parak‍kamma tapau karaumi’’||

184.

‘‘tvaṃ lauhitakkhau vihatantaraṃsau, alaṅkatau kappitakaisamassu|

surausitau lauhitacandanaina, gandhabbarājāva disā pabhāsasi pabhāsi (ka॰)||

185.

‘‘daividdhipattausi mahānubhāvau, sabbaihi kāmaihi samaṅgibhūtau|

pucchāmi taṃ nāgarājaitamatthaṃ, saiyyau itau kaina manussalaukau’’||

186.

‘‘aḷāra nāñ‍ñatra manussalaukā, suddhī va saṃvij‍jati saṃyamau vā|

ahañ‍ca laddhāna manussayauniṃ, kāhāmi jātimaraṇassa antaṃ’’||

187.

‘‘saṃvaccharau  mai vasatau vusitau (pī॰) tavantikai, an‍naina pānaina upaṭṭhitausmi|

āmantayitvāna palaimi nāga, cirappavuṭṭhausmi cirappavutthau asmi (pī॰) ahaṃ janinda’’||

188.

‘‘puttā ca dārā anujīvinau ca ca’nujīvinau (syā॰ pī॰), nic‍cānusiṭṭhā upatiṭṭhatai taṃ|

kac‍cin‍nu taṃ nābhisapittha nābhisaṃsittha (syā॰ pī॰) kauci, piyañhi mai dassanaṃ tuyhaṃ tuyha (pī॰) aḷāra’’||

189.

‘‘yathāpi  mātū ca pitū agārai, puttau piyau paṭivihitau vasaiyya saiyyau (pī॰)|

tataupi mayhaṃ idhamaiva saiyyau, cittañhi tai nāga mayī pasan‍naṃ’’||

190.

‘‘maṇī mamaṃ vij‍jati lauhitaṅkau lauhitaṅgau (ka॰), dhanāharau maṇiratanaṃ uḷāraṃ|

ādāya tvaṃ taṃ (pī॰) gaccha sakaṃ nikaitaṃ, laddhā dhanaṃ taṃ maṇimaussajassu’’||

191.

‘‘diṭṭhā mayā mānusakāpi kāmā, asassatā vipariṇāmadhammā|

ādīnavaṃ kāmaguṇaisu disvā, saddhāyahaṃ pabbajitaumhi rāja||

192.

‘‘dumapphalānīva  patanti māṇavā, daharā ca vuddhā ca sarīrabhaidā|

aitampi disvā pabbajitaumhi rāja, apaṇṇakaṃ sāmañ‍ñamaiva saiyyau’’||

193.

‘‘addhā havai saivitabbā sapañ‍ñā, bahussutā yai bahuṭhānacintinau|

nāgañ‍ca sutvāna tavañ‍caḷāra, kāhāmi puñ‍ñāni anappakāni’’||

194.

‘‘addhā havai saivitabbā sapañ‍ñā, bahussutā yai bahuṭhānacintinau|

nāgañ‍ca sutvāna mamañ‍ca rāja, karauhi puñ‍ñāni anappakānī’’ti||

saṅkhapālajātakaṃ catutthaṃ|

525. cūḷasutasaumajātakaṃ (5)

195.

‘‘āmantayāmi  nigamaṃ, mittāmac‍cai parissajai pārisaj‍jai (sī॰ syā॰)|

sirasmiṃ palitaṃ jātaṃ, pabbaj‍jaṃ dāni raucahaṃ’’||

196.

‘‘abhummai kathaṃ nu bhaṇasi, sal‍laṃ mai daiva urasi kappaisi kampaisi (pī॰)|

sattasatā tai bhariyā, kathaṃ  nu tai tā bhavissanti’’||

197.

‘‘pañ‍ñāyihinti aitā, daharā añ‍ñampi tā gamissanti|

saggañ‍cassa patthayānau, taina ahaṃ pabbajissāmi’’||

198.

‘‘dul‍laddhaṃ mai āsi sutasauma, yassa tai haumahaṃ mātā|

yaṃ mai vilapantiyā, anapaikkhau pabbajasi daiva||

199.

‘‘dul‍laddhaṃ mai āsi sutasauma, yaṃ taṃ ahaṃ vijāyissaṃ|

yaṃ mai vilapantiyā, anapaikkhau pabbajasi daiva’’||

200.

‘‘kau nāmaisau dhammau, sutasauma kā ca nāma pabbaj‍jā|

yaṃ nau amhai jiṇṇai, anapaikkhau pabbajasi daiva||

201.

‘‘puttāpi tuyhaṃ bahavau, daharā appattayaubbanā|

mañ‍jū taipi tai (sī॰ pī॰) taṃ apassantā, mañ‍ñai  dukkhaṃ nigacchanti’’||

202.

‘‘puttaihi ca mai aitaihi, daharaihi appattayaubbanaihi|

mañ‍jūhi sabbaihipi tumhaihi, cirampi ṭhatvā vināsabhāvau’’ vinābhāvau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

203.

‘‘chin‍naṃ  nu tuyhaṃ hadayaṃ, adu tai ādu (sī॰ pī॰), ādū (syā॰) karuṇā ca natthi amhaisu|

yaṃ nau vikandantiyau vik‍kandantiyau (sī॰), anapaikkhau pabbajasi daiva’’||

204.

‘‘na ca mayhaṃ chin‍naṃ hadayaṃ, atthi karuṇāpi mayhaṃ tumhaisu|

saggañ‍ca patthayānau, taina ahaṃ taināhaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), tainamahaṃ (pī॰) pabbajissāmi’’||

205.

‘‘dul‍laddhaṃ mai āsi, sutasauma yassa tai ahaṃ bhariyā|

yaṃ mai vilapantiyā, anapaikkhau pabbajasi daiva||

206.

‘‘dul‍laddhaṃ mai āsi, sutasauma yassa tai ahaṃ bhariyā|

yaṃ mai kucchipaṭisandhiṃ maṃ kucchimatiṃ santiṃ (pī॰), anapaikkhau  pabbajasi daiva||

207.

‘‘paripak‍kau mai gabbhau, kucchigatau yāva naṃ vijāyāmi|

māhaṃ aikā vidhavā, pacchā dukkhāni addakkhiṃ’’||

208.

‘‘paripak‍kau tai gabbhau, kucchigatau iṅgha tvaṃ tva (sī॰), naṃ (pī॰) vijāyassu|

puttaṃ anaumavaṇṇaṃ, taṃ hitvā pabbajissāmi’’||

209.

‘‘mā  tvaṃ candai rudi, mā sauci vanatimiramattakkhi|

ārauha varapāsādaṃ ca pāsādaṃ (pī॰), anapaikkhau ahaṃ gamissāmi’’||

210.

‘‘kau  taṃ amma kaupaisi, kiṃ raudasi paikkhasi ca maṃ bāḷhaṃ|

kaṃ avajjhaṃ ghātaimi ghātaimi kaṃ avajjhaṃ (pī॰), taṃ avajjhaṃ ghātaimi (ka॰), ñātīnaṃ udikkhamānānaṃ’’||

211.

‘‘na hi sau sak‍kā hantuṃ, vijitāvī jīvitāvī (pī॰) yau maṃ tāta kaupaisi|

pitā tai maṃ tāta avaca, anapaikkhau  ahaṃ gamissāmi’’||

212.

‘‘yauhaṃ pubbai niyyāmi, uyyānaṃ mattakuñ‍jarai ca yaudhaimi|

sutasaumai pabbajitai, kathaṃ nu dāni karissāmi’’||

213.

‘‘mātuc‍ca mātu ca (sī॰ syā॰) mai rudantyā rudatyā (pī॰), jaiṭṭhassa ca bhātunau akāmassa|

hatthaipi tai gahaissaṃ, na hi gacchasi gañchisi (pī॰) nau akāmānaṃ’’||

214.

‘‘uṭṭhaihi tvaṃ dhāti, imaṃ kumāraṃ ramaihi añ‍ñattha|

mā mai paripanthamakāsi makā (sī॰ pī॰), saggaṃ mama patthayānassa’’||

215.

‘‘yaṃ nūnimaṃ dadaiyyaṃ jahaiyyaṃ (pī॰) pabhaṅkaraṃ, kau nu mai iminātthau kau nu mai iminā atthau (sī॰ syā॰), kau nu mai natthau (pī॰)|

sutasaumai pabbajitai, kiṃ nu mainaṃ karissāmi’’||

216.

‘‘kausau  ca tuyhaṃ vipulau, kauṭṭhāgārañ‍ca tuyhaṃ paripūraṃ|

pathavī ca tuyhaṃ vijitā, ramassu  mā pabbaji pabbajassu (sī॰), pabbaja (pī॰) daiva’’||

217.

‘‘kausau ca mayhaṃ vipulau, kauṭṭhāgārañ‍ca mayhaṃ paripūraṃ|

pathavī ca mayhaṃ vijitā, taṃ hitvā pabbajissāmi’’||

218.

‘‘mayhampi dhanaṃ pahūtaṃ, saṅkhātuṃ saṅkhyātuṃ (sī॰) naupi daiva sak‍kaumi|

taṃ tai dadāmi sabbampi taṃ daiva tai dadāmi sabbampi (sī॰), taṃ tai dadāmi sabbaṃ (pī॰), ramassu mā pabbaji daiva’’||

219.

‘‘jānāmi jānāmi tai (sī॰ syā॰) dhanaṃ pahūtaṃ, kulavaddhana pūjitau tayā casmi|

saggañ‍ca patthayānau, taina ahaṃ pabbajissāmi’’||

220.

‘‘uk‍kaṇṭhitausmi bāḷhaṃ, arati maṃ saumadatta āvisati āvīsati (ka॰)|

bahukāpi bahukā hi (sī॰ syā॰) mai antarāyā, aj‍jaivāhaṃ pabbajissāmi’’||

221.

‘‘idañ‍ca tuyhaṃ rucitaṃ, sutasauma aj‍jaiva dāni tvaṃ pabbaja|

ahampi pabbajissāmi, na  ussahai tayā vinā ahaṃ ṭhātuṃ’’||

222.

‘‘na hi sak‍kā pabbajituṃ, nagarai na hi pac‍cati janapadai ca’’|

‘‘sutasaumai pabbajitai, kathaṃ nu dāni karissāma’’||

223.

‘‘upanīyatidaṃ mañ‍ñai, parittaṃ udakaṃva caṅkavāramhi|

aivaṃ suparittakai jīvitai, na ca pamaj‍jituṃ kālau||

224.

‘‘upanīyatidaṃ  mañ‍ñai, parittaṃ udakaṃva caṅkavāramhi|

aivaṃ suparittakai jīvitai, andhabālā atha bālā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pamaj‍janti||

225.

‘‘tai vaḍḍhayanti nirayaṃ, tiracchānayauniñ‍ca paittivisayañ‍ca|

taṇhāya bandhanabaddhā, vaḍḍhainti asurakāyaṃ’’||

226.

‘‘ūhañ‍ñatai  rajaggaṃ, avidūrai pubbakamhi ca pupphakamhi ca (sī॰ pī॰) pāsādai|

mañ‍ñai nau kaisā chin‍nā, yasassinau  dhammarājassa’’||

227.

‘‘ayamassa pāsādau, sauvaṇṇa sauvaṇṇau (pī॰) pupphamālyavītikiṇṇau|

yahi yamhi (pī॰) manuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau itthāgāraihi||

228.

‘‘ayamassa pāsādau, sauvaṇṇapupphamālyavītikiṇṇau|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau ñātisaṅghaina||

229.

‘‘idamassa kūṭāgāraṃ, sauvaṇṇapupphamālyavītikiṇṇaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau itthāgāraihi||

230.

‘‘idamassa kūṭāgāraṃ, sauvaṇṇa sauvaṇṇaṃ (pī॰) pupphamālyavītikiṇṇaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau ñātisaṅghaina||

231.

‘‘ayamassa asaukavanikā, supupphitā sabbakālikā rammā|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau  itthāgāraihi||

232.

‘‘ayamassa asaukavanikā, supupphitā sabbakālikā rammā|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau ñātisaṅghaina||

233.

‘‘idamassa uyyānaṃ, supupphitaṃ sabbakālikaṃ rammaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau itthāgāraihi||

234.

‘‘idamassa uyyānaṃ, supupphitaṃ sabbakālikaṃ rammaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau ñātisaṅghaina||

235.

‘‘idamassa kaṇikāravanaṃ, supupphitaṃ sabbakālikaṃ rammaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau itthāgāraihi||

236.

‘‘idamassa kaṇikāravanaṃ, supupphitaṃ sabbakālikaṃ rammaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau  ñātisaṅghaina||

237.

‘‘idamassa pāṭalivanaṃ pāṭalīvanaṃ (sī॰), supupphitaṃ sabbakālikaṃ rammaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau itthāgāraihi||

238.

‘‘idamassa pāṭalivanaṃ, supupphitaṃ sabbakālikaṃ rammaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau ñātisaṅghaina||

239.

‘‘idamassa  ambavanaṃ, supupphitaṃ sabbakālikaṃ rammaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau itthāgāraihi||

240.

‘‘idamassa ambavanaṃ, supupphitaṃ sabbakālikaṃ rammaṃ|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau ñātisaṅghaina||

241.

‘‘ayamassa paukkharaṇī, sañchan‍nā aṇḍajaihi vītikiṇṇā|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau  itthāgāraihi||

242.

‘‘ayamassa paukkharaṇī, sañchan‍nā aṇḍajaihi vītikiṇṇā|

yahimanuvicari rājā, parikiṇṇau ñātisaṅghaina’’||

243.

‘‘rājā vau khau rājā khau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) pabbajitau, sutasaumau raj‍jaṃ imaṃ pahatvāna pahantvāna (syā॰ ka॰)|

kāsāyavatthavasanau, nāgauva aikakau aikakauva (sī॰) carati’’||

244.

‘‘māssu pubbai ratikīḷitāni, hasitāni ca anussarittha anussaritthau (pī॰)|

mā vau kāmā haniṃsu, rammaṃ hi surammañhi (syā॰ ka॰) sudassanaṃ sudassanaṃ nāma (sī॰) nagaraṃ||

245.

‘‘maittacittañ‍ca maittañ‍ca (pī॰) bhāvaitha, appamāṇaṃ divā ca rattau ca|

agacchittha atha gañchittha (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) daivapura, āvāsaṃ puñ‍ñakammina’’nti puñ‍ñakammānanti (pī॰)||

cūḷasutasaumajātakaṃ pañ‍camaṃ|

cattālīsanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

suvapaṇḍitajambukakuṇḍalinau, varakañ‍ñamalambusajātakañ‍ca|

pavaruttamasaṅkhasirīvhayakau, sutasaumaarindhamarājavarau||


18. paṇṇāsanipātau

526. niḷinikājātakaṃ (1)

1.

‘‘uddayhatai  uḍḍayhatai (sī॰ pī॰) janapadau, raṭṭhañ‍cāpi vinassati|

aihi niḷinikai niḷikai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), aivamuparipi gaccha, taṃ mai brāhmaṇamānaya’’||

2.

‘‘nāhaṃ dukkhakkhamā rāja, nāhaṃ addhānakauvidā|

kathaṃ ahaṃ gamissāmi, vanaṃ kuñ‍jarasaivitaṃ’’||

3.

‘‘phītaṃ janapadaṃ gantvā, hatthinā ca rathaina ca|

dārusaṅghāṭayānaina, aivaṃ gaccha niḷinikai||

4.

‘‘hatthiassarathai pattī, gacchaivādāya khattiyai|

tavaiva vaṇṇarūpaina, vasaṃ tamānayissasi’’||

5.

‘‘kadalīdhajapañ‍ñāṇau, ābhujīparivāritau|

aisau padissati rammau, isisiṅgassa assamau||

6.

‘‘aisau aggissa saṅkhātau, aisau dhūmau padissati|

mañ‍ñai nau aggiṃ hāpaiti, isisiṅgau mahiddhikau’’||

7.

‘‘tañ‍ca  disvāna āyantiṃ, āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalaṃ|

isisiṅgau pāvisi bhītau, assamaṃ paṇṇachādanaṃ||

8.

‘‘assamassa ca sā dvārai, gaiṇḍukainassa bhaiṇḍukainassa (sī॰ pī॰) kīḷati|

vidaṃsayantī aṅgāni, guyhaṃ pakāsitāni ca||

9.

‘‘tañ‍ca disvāna kīḷantiṃ, paṇṇasālagatau jaṭī|

assamā nikkhamitvāna, idaṃ vacanamabravi||

10.

‘‘ambhau kau nāma sau rukkhau, yassa taivaṃgataṃ phalaṃ|

dūraipi khittaṃ pac‍caiti, na taṃ auhāya gacchati’’||

11.

‘‘assamassa  mama maṃ (sī॰) brahmai, samīpai gandhamādanai|

bahavau pabbatai (sī॰) tādisā rukkhā, yassa taivaṃgataṃ phalaṃ|

dūraipi khittaṃ pac‍caiti, na maṃ auhāya gacchati’’||

12.

‘‘aitū  aitu (sī॰ syā॰ ka॰) bhavaṃ assamimaṃ adaitu, paj‍jañ‍ca bhakkhañ‍ca paṭiccha dammi|

idamāsanaṃ atra bhavaṃ nisīdatu, itau bhavaṃ mūlaphalāni bhuñ‍jatu’’ khādatu (sī॰)||

13.

‘‘kiṃ tai idaṃ ūrūnamantarasmiṃ, supicchitaṃ kaṇharivappakāsati|

akkhāhi mai pucchitau aitamatthaṃ, kausai nu tai uttamaṅgaṃ paviṭṭhaṃ’’||

14.

‘‘ahaṃ vanai mūlaphalaisanaṃ caraṃ, āsādayiṃ  assādayiṃ (ka॰) acchaṃ sughaurarūpaṃ|

sau maṃ patitvā sahasājjhapattau, panuj‍ja maṃ abbahi abbuhi (syā॰ ka॰) uttamaṅgaṃ||

15.

‘‘svāyaṃ vaṇau khaj‍jati kaṇḍuvāyati, sabbañ‍ca kālaṃ na labhāmi sātaṃ|

pahau bhavaṃ kaṇḍumimaṃ vinaituṃ, kurutaṃ bhavaṃ yācitau brāhmaṇatthaṃ’’||

16.

‘‘gambhīrarūpau tai vaṇau salauhitau, apūtikau vaṇagandhau pak‍kagandhau (sī॰), pan‍nagandhau (syā॰ pī॰) mahā ca|

karaumi tai kiñ‍ci kasāyayaugaṃ, yathā bhavaṃ paramasukhī bhavaiyya’’||

17.

‘‘na  mantayaugā na kasāyayaugā, na ausadhā brahmacāri brahmacārī (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) kamanti|

ghaṭṭai mudukaina yaṃ tai mudu taina (sī॰), yaṃ tai mudū taina (pī॰) vinaihi kaṇḍuṃ kaṇḍukaṃ (pī॰), yathā ahaṃ paramasukhī bhavaiyyaṃ’’||

18.

‘‘itau nu bhautau katamaina assamau, kac‍ci bhavaṃ abhiramasi abhiramasī (pī॰) arañ‍ñai|

kac‍ci nu tai kac‍ci tai (pī॰) mūlaphalaṃ pahūtaṃ, kac‍ci bhavantaṃ na vihiṃsanti vāḷā’’||

19.

‘‘itau  ujuṃ uttarāyaṃ disāyaṃ, khaimānadī himavatā pabhāvī pabhāti (sī॰ pī॰)|

tassā tīrai assamau mayha rammau, ahau bhavaṃ assamaṃ mayhaṃ passai||

20.

‘‘ambā ca sālā tilakā ca jambuyau, uddālakā pāṭaliyau ca phul‍lā|

samantatau kimpurisābhigītaṃ, ahau bhavaṃ assamaṃ mayhaṃ passai||

21.

‘‘tālā ca mūlā ca phalā ca maittha, vaṇṇaina gandhaina upaitarūpaṃ|

taṃ bhūmibhāgaihi upaitarūpaṃ, ahau bhavaṃ assamaṃ mayhaṃ passai||

21.

‘‘phalā ca mūlā ca pahūtamaittha, vaṇṇaina gandhaina rasainupaitā|

āyanti ca luddakā taṃ padaisaṃ, mā mai tatau mūlaphalaṃ ahāsuṃ’’||

23.

‘‘pitā mamaṃ mūlaphalaisanaṃ gatau, idāni āgacchati sāyakālai|

ubhauva gacchāmasai assamaṃ taṃ, yāva  pitā mūlaphalatau aitu’’||

24.

‘‘añ‍ñai  bahū isayau sādhurūpā, rājīsayau anumaggai vasanti|

tai yaiva pucchaisi mamassamaṃ taṃ, tai taṃ nayissanti mamaṃ sakāsai’’||

25.

‘‘na tai kaṭṭhāni bhin‍nāni, na tai udakamābhataṃ|

aggīpi tai na hāpitau hāsitau (sī॰ syā॰), kiṃ nu mandauva jhāyasi||

26.

‘‘bhin‍nāni kaṭṭhāni hutau ca aggi, tapanīpi tai samitā brahmacārī brahmacāri (?)|

pīṭhañ‍ca mayhaṃ udakañ‍ca hauti, ramasi tuvaṃ tvaṃ (sī॰) brahmabhūtau puratthā||

27.

‘‘abhin‍nakaṭṭhausi  anābhataudakau, ahāpitaggīsi ahāpitaggīpi (ka॰) asiddhabhaujanau asiṭṭhabhaujanau (ka॰)|

na mai tuvaṃ ālapasī mamaj‍ja, naṭṭhaṃ nu kiṃ caitasikañ‍ca dukkhaṃ’’||

28.

‘‘idhāgamā jaṭilau brahmacārī, sudassanaiyyau sutanū vinaiti|

naivātidīghau na panātirassau, sukaṇhakaṇhacchadanaihi bhautau||

29.

‘‘amassujātau  apurāṇavaṇṇī, ādhārarūpañ‍ca panassa kaṇṭhai|

dvai yamā dvai passa (sī॰), dvāssa (pī॰) gaṇḍā uraisu jātā, suvaṇṇatindukanibhā suvaṇṇapindūpanibhā (sī॰), suvaṇṇatiṇḍusan‍nibhā (syā॰), sauvan‍napiṇḍūpanibhā (pī॰) pabhassarā||

30.

‘‘mukhañ‍ca tassa bhusadassanaiyyaṃ, kaṇṇaisu lambanti ca kuñ‍citaggā|

tai jautarai caratau māṇavassa, suttañ‍ca yaṃ saṃyamanaṃ jaṭānaṃ||

31.

‘‘añ‍ñā  ca tassa saṃyamāni saṃyamanī (sī॰ pī॰) catassau, nīlā pītā nīlāpi tā (pī॰) lauhitikā lauhitakā (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) ca saitā|

tā piṃsarai saṃsarai (sī॰ syā॰) caratau māṇavassa, tiriṭi cirīṭi (sī॰ pī॰) saṅghāriva pāvusamhi||

32.

‘‘na mikhalaṃ muñ‍jamayaṃ dhāraiti, na santharai santacai (sī॰), santacaṃ (pī॰), santarai (ka॰) nau pana pabbajassa|

tā jautarai jaghanantarai jaghanavarai (sī॰ pī॰) vilaggā, satairatā vij‍jurivantalikkhai||

33.

‘‘akhīlakāni ca avaṇṭakāni, haiṭṭhā nabhyā kaṭisamauhitāni|

aghaṭṭitā nic‍cakīḷaṃ karaunti, haṃ tāta kiṃrukkhaphalāni tāni||

34.

‘‘jaṭā  ca tassa bhusadassanaiyyā, parausataṃ vail‍litaggā sugandhā|

dvaidhā sirau sādhu vibhattarūpau, ahau nu khau mayha tathā jaṭāssu||

35.

‘‘yadā ca sau pakirati tā jaṭāyau, vaṇṇaina gandhaina upaitarūpā|

nīluppalaṃ vātasamairitaṃva, tathaiva saṃvāti panassamau ayaṃ||

36.

‘‘paṅkau ca tassa bhusadassanaiyyau, naitādisau yādisau mayhaṃ kāyai kāyau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

sau vāyatī airitau mālutaina, vanaṃ yathā aggagimhai suphul‍laṃ||

37.

‘‘nihanti  sau rukkhaphalaṃ pathabyā, sucittarūpaṃ ruciraṃ dassanaiyyaṃ|

khittañ‍ca tassa punaraiti hatthaṃ, haṃ tāta kiṃrukkhaphalaṃ nu khau taṃ||

38.

‘‘dantā ca tassa bhusadassanaiyyā, suddhā samā saṅkhavarūpapan‍nā|

manau pasādainti vivariyamānā, na hi na ha (sī॰ pī॰) nūna sau sākamakhādi taihi||

39.

‘‘akak‍kasaṃ  aggaḷitaṃ muhuṃ muduṃ, ujuṃ anuddhataṃ acapalamassa bhāsitaṃ|

rudaṃ manuñ‍ñaṃ karavīkasussaraṃ, hadayaṅgamaṃ rañ‍jayataiva mai manau||

40.

‘‘bindussarau nātivisaṭṭhavākyau nātivissaṭṭhavākyau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), na nūna sajjhāyamatippayuttau|

icchāmi bhau khau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) taṃ punadaiva daṭṭhuṃ, mittau hi mittaṃ hi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) mai māṇavauhu māṇavāhu (sī॰ syā॰), māṇavāhū (pī॰) puratthā||

41.

‘‘susandhi  sabbattha vimaṭṭhimaṃ vaṇaṃ, puthū puthuṃ (pī॰), puthu (ka॰) sujātaṃ kharapattasan‍nibhaṃ|

tainaiva maṃ uttariyāna māṇavau, vivaritaṃ ūruṃ jaghanaina pīḷayi||

42.

‘‘tapanti ābhanti viraucarai ca, satairatā vij‍jurivantalikkhai|

bāhā mudū añ‍janalaumasādisā, vicitravaṭṭaṅgulikāssa saubharai||

43.

‘‘akak‍kasaṅgau  na ca dīghalaumau, nakhāssa dīghā api lauhitaggā|

mudūhi bāhāhi palissajantau, kalyāṇarūpau ramayaṃ ramayhaṃ (ka॰) upaṭṭhahi||

44.

‘‘dumassa  tūlūpanibhā pabhassarā, suvaṇṇakambutalavaṭṭasucchavī|

hatthā mudū taihi maṃ saṃphusitvā, itau gatau taina maṃ dahanti tāta||

45.

‘‘na nūna na ha nūna (sī॰ pī॰) sau khārividhaṃ ahāsi, na nūna sau kaṭṭhāni sayaṃ abhañ‍ji|

na nūna sau hanti dumai kuṭhāriyā kudhāriyā (ka॰), na hissa na pissa (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) hatthaisu khilāni atthi||

46.

‘‘acchau ca khau tassa vaṇaṃ akāsi, sau maṃbravi sukhitaṃ maṃ karauhi|

tāhaṃ kariṃ taina mamāsi saukhyaṃ, sau cabravi sukhitausmīti brahmai||

47.

‘‘ayañ‍ca tai māluvapaṇṇasanthatā, vikiṇṇarūpāva mayā ca taina ca|

kilantarūpā udakai ramitvā, punappunaṃ paṇṇakuṭiṃ vajāma||

48.

‘‘na maj‍ja mantā paṭibhanti tāta, na aggihuttaṃ napi yañ‍ñatantaṃ yañ‍ñatantraṃ (sī॰), yañ‍ñaṃ tatra (pī॰ ka॰), yañ‍ñatatra (syā॰)|

na cāpi tai mūlaphalāni bhuñ‍jai, yāva  na passāmi taṃ brahmacāriṃ||

49.

‘‘addhā  pajānāsi tuvampi tāta, yassaṃ disaṃ disāyaṃ (syā॰ pī॰ ka॰) vasatai brahmacārī|

taṃ maṃ disaṃ pāpaya tāta khippaṃ, mā tai ahaṃ amarimassamamhi||

50.

‘‘vicitraphul‍laṃ vicitrapupphaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) hi vanaṃ sutaṃ mayā, dijābhighuṭṭhaṃ dijasaṅghasaivitaṃ|

taṃ maṃ vanaṃ pāpaya tāta khippaṃ, purā tai pāṇaṃ vijahāmi assamai’’||

51.

‘‘imasmāhaṃ jautirasai vanamhi, gandhabbadaivaccharasaṅghasaivitai|

isīnamāvāsai sanantanamhi, naitādisaṃ aratiṃ pāpuṇaitha||

52.

‘‘bhavanti mittāni athau na haunti, ñātīsu mittaisu karaunti paimaṃ|

ayañ‍ca jammau kissa vā niviṭṭhau, yau naiva jānāti kutaumhi āgatau||

53.

‘‘saṃvāsaina hi mittāni, sandhiyanti sandhīyanti (sī॰ pī॰) punappunaṃ|

svaiva mittau sā ca maitti (pī॰) asaṃgantu, asaṃvāsaina jīrati||

54.

‘‘sacai  tuvaṃ dakkhasi brahmacāriṃ, sacai tuvaṃ sal‍lapai sal‍lapi (sī॰) brahmacārinā|

sampan‍nasassaṃva mahaudakaina, tapauguṇaṃ khippamimaṃ pahissasi pahassasi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)||

55.

‘‘punapi  punappi (pī॰) cai dakkhasi brahmacāriṃ, punapi punappi (pī॰) cai sal‍lapai brahmacārinā|

sampan‍nasassaṃva mahaudakaina, usmāgataṃ khippamimaṃ pahissasi||

56.

‘‘bhūtāni  haitāni aitāni (pī॰) caranti tāta, virūparūpaina manussalaukai|

na tāni saivaitha narau sapañ‍ñau, āsaj‍ja naṃ nassati brahmacārī’’ti||

niḷinikājātakaṃ naḷinījātakaṃ (sī॰), naḷinijātakaṃ (pī॰) paṭhamaṃ|

527. ummādantījātakaṃ (2)

57.

‘‘nivaisanaṃ kassa nudaṃ sunanda, pākāraina paṇḍumayaina guttaṃ|

kā dissati aggisikhāva dūrai, vaihāyasaṃ vaihāsayaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) pabbataggaiva ac‍ci||

58.

‘‘dhītā  nvayaṃ nayaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), nvāyaṃ (syā॰) kassa sunanda hauti, suṇisā nvayaṃ nayaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), nvāyaṃ (syā॰) kassa athaupi bhariyā|

akkhāhi mai khippamidhaiva puṭṭhau, avāvaṭā yadi vā atthi bhattā’’||

59.

‘‘ahañhi jānāmi janinda aitaṃ, matyā ca paityā ca athaupi assā|

tavaiva sau purisau bhūmipāla, rattindivaṃ appamattau tavatthai||

60.

‘‘iddhau ca phītau ca suvaḍḍhitau subāḷhikau (pī॰) ca, amac‍cau ca tai añ‍ñatarau janinda|

tassaisā bhariyābhipārakassa ahipārakassa (sī॰ pī॰), abhipādakassa (ka॰), ummādantī ummādantīti (ka॰) nāmadhaiyyaina rāja’’||

61.

‘‘ambhau  ambhau nāmamidaṃ imissā, matyā ca paityā ca kataṃ susādhu|

tadā tathā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) hi mayhaṃ avalaukayantī, ummattakaṃ ummadantī akāsi’’||

62.

‘‘yā puṇṇamāsai puṇṇamāyai (ka॰) migamandalaucanā, upāvisi puṇḍarīkattacaṅgī|

dvai puṇṇamāyau tadahū amañ‍ñahaṃ, disvāna pārāvatarattavāsiniṃ||

63.

‘‘aḷārapamhaihi  subhaihi vaggubhi, palaubhayantī maṃ yadā udikkhati|

vijambhamānā harataiva mai manau, jātā vanai kimpurisīva pabbatai||

64.

‘‘tadā hi brahatī sāmā, āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā|

aikac‍cavasanā nārī, migī bhantāvudikkhati||

65.

‘‘kadāssu maṃ tambanakhā sulaumā, bāhāmudū candanasāralittā|

vaṭṭaṅgulī san‍natadhīrakuttiyā, nārī upañ‍ñissati sīsatau subhā||

66.

‘‘kadāssu maṃ kañ‍canajāluracchadā, dhītā tirīṭissa vilaggamajjhā|

mudūhi bāhāhi palissajissati, brahāvanai jātadumaṃva māluvā||

67.

‘‘kadāssu  kadāssu maṃ (syā॰ ka॰) lākhārasarattasucchavī, bindutthanī puṇḍarīkattacaṅgī|

mukhaṃ mukhaina upanāmayissati, sauṇḍauva sauṇḍassa surāya thālaṃ||

68.

‘‘yadāddasaṃ yathāddasaṃ (pī॰) taṃ tiṭṭhantiṃ, sabbabhaddaṃ sabbagattaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) manauramaṃ|

tatau  sakassa cittassa, nāvabaudhāmi kañ‍cinaṃ kiñ‍cinaṃ (ka॰), kiñ‍canaṃ (pī॰)||

69.

‘‘ummādantimahaṃ  daṭṭhā diṭṭhā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰), āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalaṃ|

na supāmi divārattiṃ, sahassaṃva parājitau||

70.

‘‘sak‍kau cai ca (sī॰ pī॰) mai varaṃ daj‍jā, sau ca labbhaitha mai varau|

aikarattaṃ dvirattaṃ dirattaṃ (pī॰) vā, bhavaiyyaṃ abhipārakau|

ummādantyā ramitvāna, sivirājā tatau siyaṃ’’ siyā (syā॰ pī॰)||

71.

‘‘bhūtāni mai bhūtapatī namassatau, āgamma yakkhau idamaitadabravi|

rañ‍ñau manau ummadantyā niviṭṭhau, dadāmi tai taṃ paricārayassu’’||

72.

‘‘puñ‍ñā vidhaṃsai amarau na camhi, janau ca mai pāpamidañ‍ca pāpamidanti (sī॰ pī॰) jañ‍ñā|

bhusau ca tyassa manasau vighātau, datvā piyaṃ ummadantiṃ adaṭṭhā’’||

73.

‘‘janinda nāñ‍ñatra tayā mayā vā, sabbāpi kammassa katassa jañ‍ñā|

yaṃ tai mayā ummadantī padin‍nā, bhusaihi rājā vanathaṃ sajāhi’’||

74.

‘‘yau pāpakaṃ kamma karaṃ manussau, sau  mañ‍ñati māyida māyidha (ka॰) mañ‍ñiṃsu añ‍ñai|

passanti bhūtāni karauntamaitaṃ, yuttā ca yai haunti narā pathabyā||

75.

‘‘añ‍ñau nu tai kauci kaudha (pī॰) narau pathabyā, saddhaiyya saddahaiyya (sī॰) laukasmi na mai piyāti|

bhusau ca tyassa manasau vighātau, datvā piyaṃ ummadantiṃ adaṭṭhā’’||

76.

‘‘addhā  piyā mayha janinda aisā, na sā mamaṃ appiyā bhūmipāla|

gacchaiva tvaṃ ummadantiṃ bhadantai, sīhauva sailassa guhaṃ upaiti’’||

77.

‘‘na pīḷitā attadukhaina dhīrā, sukhapphalaṃ kamma paric‍cajanti|

sammauhitā vāpi sukhaina mattā, na pāpakammañ‍ca pāpakaṃ kamma (pī॰) samācaranti’’||

78.

‘‘tuvañhi mātā ca pitā ca mayhaṃ, bhattā patī pausakau daivatā ca|

dāsau ahaṃ tuyha saputtadārau, yathāsukhaṃ sāmi sibba (sī॰), sīvi (syā॰) karauhi kāmaṃ’’||

79.

‘‘yau issaraumhīti karauti pāpaṃ, katvā  ca sau nuttasatai nuttapatai (pī॰) paraisaṃ|

na taina sau jīvati dīghamāyu dīghamāyuṃ (sī॰ syā॰), daivāpi pāpaina samaikkharai naṃ||

80.

‘‘añ‍ñātakaṃ sāmikaihī padin‍naṃ, dhammai ṭhitā yai paṭicchanti dānaṃ|

paṭicchakā dāyakā cāpi tattha, sukhapphalañ‍ñaiva karaunti kammaṃ’’||

81.

‘‘añ‍ñau  nu tai kauci narau pathabyā, saddhaiyya laukasmi na mai piyāti|

bhusau ca tyassa manasau vighātau, datvā piyaṃ ummadantiṃ adaṭṭhā’’||

82.

‘‘addhā piyā mayha janinda aisā, na sā mamaṃ appiyā bhūmipāla|

yaṃ tai mayā ummadantī padin‍nā, bhusaihi rājā vanathaṃ sajāhi’’||

83.

‘‘yau  attadukkhaina parassa dukkhaṃ, sukhaina vā attasukhaṃ dahāti|

yathaividaṃ mayha tathā paraisaṃ, yau sau (pī॰) aivaṃ jānāti pajānāti (ka॰) sa vaidi dhammaṃ||

84.

‘‘añ‍ñau nu tai kauci narau pathabyā, saddhaiyya  laukasmi na mai piyāti|

bhusau ca tyassa manasau vighātau, datvā piyaṃ ummadantiṃ adaṭṭhā’’||

85.

‘‘janinda jānāsi piyā mamaisā, na sā mamaṃ appiyā bhūmipāla|

piyaina tai dammi piyaṃ janinda, piyadāyinau daiva piyaṃ labhanti’’||

86.

‘‘sau nūnāhaṃ vadhissāmi, attānaṃ kāmahaitukaṃ|

na hi dhammaṃ adhammaina, ahaṃ vadhitumussahai’’||

87.

‘‘sacai tuvaṃ mayha satiṃ santi (ka॰) janinda, na kāmayāsi naravīra saiṭṭha|

cajāmi naṃ sabbajanassa sibyā sibba (sī॰ pī॰), majjhai (syā॰), mayā pamuttaṃ tatau avhayaisi avhayāsi (ka॰) naṃ’’||

88.

‘‘adūsiyaṃ cai abhipāraka tvaṃ, cajāsi kattai ahitāya tyassa|

mahā ca tai upavādaupi assa, na cāpi tyassa nagaramhi pakkhau’’||

89.

‘‘ahaṃ sahissaṃ upavādamaitaṃ, nindaṃ pasaṃsaṃ garahañ‍ca sabbaṃ|

mamaitamāgacchatu bhūmipāla, yathāsukhaṃ  sivi sibba (sī॰ pī॰) karauhi kāmaṃ’’||

90.

‘‘yau  naiva nindaṃ na panappasaṃsaṃ, ādiyati garahaṃ naupi pūjaṃ|

sirī ca lakkhī ca apaiti tamhā, āpau suvuṭṭhīva yathā thalamhā’’||

91.

‘‘yaṃ kiñ‍ci dukkhañ‍ca sukhañ‍ca aittau, dhammātisārañ‍ca manauvighātaṃ|

urasā ahaṃ pac‍cuttarissāmi paṭicchissāmi (sī॰ syā॰), pac‍cupadissāmi (pī॰) sabbaṃ, pathavī yathā thāvarānaṃ tasānaṃ’’||

92.

‘‘dhammātisārañ‍ca manauvighātaṃ, dukkhañ‍ca nicchāmi ahaṃ paraisaṃ|

aikauvimaṃ hārayissāmi bhāraṃ, dhammai ṭhitau kiñ‍ci ahāpayantau’’||

93.

‘‘saggūpagaṃ puñ‍ñakammaṃ janinda, mā mai tuvaṃ antarāyaṃ akāsi|

dadāmi tai ummadantiṃ pasan‍nau, rājāva yañ‍ñai dhanaṃ brāhmaṇānaṃ’’||

94.

‘‘addhā tuvaṃ kattai hitaisi mayhaṃ, sakhā mamaṃ ummadantī tuvañ‍ca|

nindaiyyu daivā pitarau ca sabbai, pāpañ‍ca  passaṃ abhisamparāyaṃ’’||

95.

‘‘na haitadhammaṃ sivirāja vaj‍juṃ, sanaigamā jānapadā ca sabbai|

yaṃ tai mayā ummadantī padin‍nā, bhusaihi rājā vanathaṃ sajāhi’’||

96.

‘‘addhā  tuvaṃ kattai hitaisi mayhaṃ, sakhā mamaṃ ummadantī tuvañ‍ca|

satañ‍ca dhammāni sukittitāni, samuddavailāva durac‍cayāni’’||

97.

‘‘āhunaiyyau  maisi hitānukampī, dhātā vidhātā casi kāmapālau|

tayī hutā rāja mahapphalā hi mahapphalā hi mai (pī॰), kāmaina mai ummadantiṃ paṭiccha’’||

98.

‘‘addhā hi sabbaṃ abhipāraka tvaṃ, dhammaṃ acārī mama kattuputta|

añ‍ñau nu tai kau idha sautthikattā, dvipadau narau aruṇai jīvalaukai’’||

99.

‘‘tuvaṃ nu saiṭṭhau tvamanuttarausi, tvaṃ dhammagū dhammaguttau (sī॰) dhammavidū sumaidhau|

sau dhammaguttau ciramaiva jīva, dhammañ‍ca  mai daisaya dhammapāla’’||

100.

‘‘tadiṅgha abhipāraka, suṇauhi vacanaṃ mama|

dhammaṃ tai daisayissāmi, sataṃ āsaivitaṃ ahaṃ||

101.

‘‘sādhu dhammaruci rājā, sādhu pañ‍ñāṇavā narau|

sādhu mittānamaddubbhau, pāpassākaraṇaṃ sukhaṃ||

102.

‘‘ak‍kaudhanassa vijitai, ṭhitadhammassa rājinau|

sukhaṃ manussā āsaitha, sītacchāyāya saṅgharai||

103.

‘‘na cāhamaitaṃ abhiraucayāmi, kammaṃ asamaikkhakataṃ asādhu|

yai vāpi ñatvāna sayaṃ karaunti, upamā imā mayhaṃ tuvaṃ suṇauhi||

104.

‘‘gavaṃ cai taramānānaṃ, jimhaṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā tā jimhaṃ gacchanti, naittai jimhaṃ gatai sati||

105.

‘‘aivamaiva aivamaivaṃ (pī॰) manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau cai adhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ  raṭṭhaṃ dukhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti adhammikau||

106.

‘‘gavaṃ  cai taramānānaṃ, ujuṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā gāvī ujuṃ yanti, naittai ujuṃ gatai sati||

107.

‘‘aivamaiva manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau sacai dhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ sukhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti dhammikau||

108.

‘‘na cāpāhaṃ adhammaina, amarattamabhipatthayai|

imaṃ vā pathaviṃ sabbaṃ, vijaituṃ abhipāraka||

109.

‘‘yañhi kiñ‍ci manussaisu, ratanaṃ idha vij‍jati|

gāvau dāsau hirañ‍ñañ‍ca, vatthiyaṃ haricandanaṃ||

110.

‘‘assitthiyau  assitthiyau ca (sī॰) ratanaṃ maṇikañ‍ca, yañ‍cāpi mai candasūriyā abhipālayanti|

na tassa haitu visamaṃ caraiyyaṃ, majjhai sivīnaṃ usabhaumhi jātau||

111.

‘‘naitā hitā naitābhi tā (sī॰) uggatau raṭṭhapālau, dhammaṃ sivīnaṃ apacāyamānau|

sau dhammamaivānuvicintayantau, tasmā sakai cittavasai na vattau’’||

112.

‘‘addhā tuvaṃ mahārāja, nic‍caṃ abyasanaṃ sivaṃ|

karissasi ciraṃ raj‍jaṃ, pañ‍ñā hi tava tādisī||

113.

‘‘aitaṃ tai anumaudāma, yaṃ dhammaṃ nappamaj‍jasi|

dhammaṃ pamaj‍ja khattiyau, raṭṭhā ṭhānā (sī॰) cavati issarau||

114.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, mātāpitūsu khattiya|

idha  dhammaṃ caritvāna, rāja saggaṃ gamissasi||

115.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, puttadāraisu khattiya…pai॰…||

116.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, mittāmac‍caisu khattiya…pai॰…||

117.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, vāhanaisu balaisu ca…pai॰…||

118.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, gāmaisu nigamaisu ca…pai॰…||

119.

‘‘dhammaṃ  cara mahārāja, raṭṭhaisu janapadaisu ca…pai॰…||

120.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, samaṇabrāhmaṇaisu ca…pai॰…||

121.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, migapakkhīsu khattiya…pai॰…||

122.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, dhammau ciṇṇau sukhāvahau|

idha dhammaṃ caritvāna, rāja saggaṃ gamissasi||

123.

‘‘dhammaṃ cara mahārāja, saindā daivā sabrahmakā|

suciṇṇaina divaṃ pattā, mā dhammaṃ rāja pāmadau’’ti||

ummādantījātakaṃ dutiyaṃ|

528. mahābaudhijātakaṃ (3)

124.

‘‘kiṃ  nu daṇḍaṃ kimajinaṃ, kiṃ chattaṃ kimupāhanaṃ|

kimaṅkusañ‍ca pattañ‍ca, saṅghāṭiñ‍cāpi brāhmaṇa|

taramānarūpauhāsi gaṇhāsi (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), kiṃ nu patthayasai disaṃ’’||

125.

‘‘dvādasaitāni  vassāni, vusitāni tavantikai|

nābhijānāmi sauṇaina, piṅgalainābhikūjitaṃ||

126.

‘‘svāyaṃ dittauva nadati, suk‍kadāṭhaṃ vidaṃsayaṃ|

tava sutvā sabhariyassa, vītasaddhassa maṃ pati’’||

127.

‘‘ahu aisa katau dausau, yathā bhāsasi brāhmaṇa|

aisa bhiyyau pasīdāmi, vasa brāhmaṇa māgamā’’||

128.

‘‘sabbasaitau purai āsi, tataupi sabalau ahu|

sabbalauhitakau dāni, kālau pak‍kamituṃ mama||

129.

‘‘abbhantaraṃ purai āsi, tatau majjhai tatau bahi|

purā niddhamanā hauti, sayamaiva vajāmahaṃ||

130.

‘‘vītasaddhaṃ na saivaiyya, udapānaṃvanaudakaṃ|

sacaipi naṃ anukhaṇai, vāri kaddamagandhikaṃ||

131.

‘‘pasan‍namaiva saivaiyya, appasan‍naṃ vivaj‍jayai|

pasan‍naṃ payirupāsaiyya, rahadaṃ vudakatthikau||

132.

‘‘bhajai  bhajantaṃ purisaṃ, abhajantaṃ na bhaj‍jayai bhājayai (pī॰)|

asappurisadhammau sau, yau bhajantaṃ na bhaj‍jati bhājati (pī॰)||

133.

‘‘yau  bhajantaṃ na bhajati, saivamānaṃ na saivati|

sa vai manussapāpiṭṭhau, migau sākhassitau yathā||

134.

‘‘ac‍cābhikkhaṇasaṃsaggā, asamausaraṇaina ca|

aitaina mittā jīranti, akālai yācanāya ca||

135.

‘‘tasmā nābhikkhaṇaṃ gacchai, na ca gacchai cirāciraṃ|

kālaina yācaṃ yācaiyya, aivaṃ mittā na jīyarai jīrarai (syā॰ pī॰)||

136.

‘‘aticiraṃ nivāsaina, piyau bhavati appiyau|

āmanta khau taṃ gacchāma, purā tai hauma appiyā’’||

137.

‘‘aivaṃ cai yācamānānaṃ, añ‍jaliṃ nāvabujjhasi|

paricārakānaṃ sataṃ paricārikānaṃ sattānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), vacanaṃ na karausi nau|

aivaṃ taṃ abhiyācāma, puna kayirāsi pariyāyaṃ’’||

138.

‘‘aivaṃ cai nau viharataṃ, antarāyau na haissati|

tuyhaṃ vāpi tumhañ‍cāpi (sī॰), tuyhañ‍cāpi (pī॰) mahārāja, mayhaṃ vā amhaṃ vā (sī॰), mayhañ‍ca (pī॰) raṭṭhavaddhana|

appaiva nāma passaima, ahaurattānamac‍cayai’’||

139.

‘‘udīraṇā  cai saṃgatyā, bhāvāya manuvattati|

akāmā akaraṇīyaṃ vā, karaṇīyaṃ vāpi kubbati|

ākāmākaraṇīyamhi, k‍vidha pāpaina lippati limpati (syā॰ ka॰)||

140.

‘‘sau cai atthau ca dhammau ca, kalyāṇau na ca pāpakau|

bhautau cai vacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, suhatau vānarau mayā||

141.

‘‘attanau cai hi vādassa, aparādhaṃ vijāniyā vijāniya (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

na  maṃ tvaṃ garahaiyyāsi, bhautau vādau hi tādisau’’||

142.

‘‘issarau  sabbalaukassa, sacai kappaiti jīvitaṃ|

iddhiṃ iddhi (pī॰ ka॰) byasanabhāvañ‍ca, kammaṃ kalyāṇapāpakaṃ|

niddaisakārī purisau, issarau taina lippati||

143.

‘‘sau cai atthau ca dhammau ca, kalyāṇau na ca pāpakau|

bhautau cai vacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, suhatau vānarau mayā||

144.

‘‘attanau cai hi vādassa, aparādhaṃ vijāniyā|

na maṃ tvaṃ garahaiyyāsi, bhautau vādau hi tādisau’’||

145.

‘‘sacai pubbaikatahaitu, sukhadukkhaṃ nigacchati|

paurāṇakaṃ kataṃ pāpaṃ, tamaisau muc‍catai muñ‍catai (sī॰ syā॰) iṇaṃ|

paurāṇakaiṇamaukkhau, k‍vidha pāpaina lippati||

146.

‘‘sau cai atthau ca dhammau ca, kalyāṇau na ca pāpakau|

bhautau cai vacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, suhatau vānarau mayā||

147.

‘‘attanau cai hi vādassa, aparādhaṃ vijāniyā|

na maṃ tvaṃ garahaiyyāsi, bhautau vādau hi tādisau’’||

148.

‘‘catun‍naṃyaivupādāya, rūpaṃ sambhauti pāṇinaṃ|

yatau ca rūpaṃ sambhauti, tatthaivānupagacchati|

idhaiva jīvati jīvau, paic‍ca paic‍ca vinassati||

149.

ucchij‍jati ayaṃ laukau, yai bālā yai ca paṇḍitā|

ucchij‍jamānai laukasmiṃ, k‍vidha pāpaina lippati||

150.

‘‘sau  cai atthau ca dhammau ca, kalyāṇau na ca pāpakau|

bhautau cai vacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, suhatau vānarau mayā||

151.

‘‘attanau cai hi vādassa, aparādhaṃ vijāniyā|

na maṃ tvaṃ garahaiyyāsi, bhautau vādau hi tādisau’’||

152.

‘‘āhu  khattavidā khattavidhā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) laukai, bālā paṇḍitamāninau||

mātaraṃ pitaraṃ hañ‍ñai, athau jaiṭṭhampi bhātaraṃ|

hanaiyya putta puttai ca (pī॰) dārai ca, atthau cai tādisau siyā||

153.

‘‘yassa  rukkhassa chāyāya, nisīdaiyya sayaiyya vā|

na tassa sākhaṃ bhañ‍jaiyya, mittadubbhau mittadūbhī (pī॰) hi pāpakau||

154.

‘‘atha atthai samuppan‍nai, samūlamapi abbahai abbhahai (syā॰ ka॰)|

atthau mai sambalaināpi, suhatau vānarau mayā||

155.

ayaṃ gāthā sīhaḷapautthakai natthi ‘‘sau cai atthau ca dhammau ca, kalyāṇau na ca pāpakau|

bhautau cai vacanaṃ sac‍caṃ, suhatau vānarau mayā ayaṃ gāthā sīhaḷapautthakai natthi||

156.

‘‘attanau cai hi vādassa, aparādhaṃ vijāniyā|

na maṃ tvaṃ garahaiyyāsi, bhautau vādau hi tādisau||

157.

‘‘ahaituvādau purisau, yau ca issarakuttikau|

pubbaikatī ca ucchaidī, yau ca khattavidau narau||

158.

‘‘aitai asappurisā laukai, bālā paṇḍitamāninau|

karaiyya tādisau pāpaṃ, athau añ‍ñampi kārayai|

asappurisasaṃsaggau, dukkhantau duk‍kaṭau (sī॰) kaṭukudrayau||

159.

‘‘urabbharūpaina vakassu bakāsu (sī॰ syā॰), vakāsu (pī॰) pubbai, asaṃkitau ajayūthaṃ upaiti|

hantvā uraṇiṃ ajikaṃ ajiyaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) ajañ‍ca, utrāsayitvā citrāsayitvā (sī॰ pī॰) yaina kāmaṃ palaiti||

160.

‘‘tathāvidhaikai samaṇabrāhmaṇāsai, chadanaṃ katvā vañ‍cayanti manussai|

anāsakā thaṇḍilasaiyyakā ca, rajaujal‍laṃ uk‍kuṭikappadhānaṃ|

pariyāyabhattañ‍ca apānakattā, pāpācārā arahantau vadānā||

161.

‘‘aitai asappurisā laukai, bālā paṇḍitamāninau|

karaiyya tādisau pāpaṃ, athau añ‍ñampi kārayai|

asappurisasaṃsaggau, dukkhantau kaṭukudrayau||

162.

‘‘yamāhu  natthi vīriyanti, ahaituñ‍ca pavadanti haituñ‍ca apavadanti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) yai|

parakāraṃ attakārañ‍ca, yai tucchaṃ samavaṇṇayuṃ||

163.

‘‘aitai asappurisā laukai, bālā paṇḍitamāninau|

karaiyya tādisau pāpaṃ, athau añ‍ñampi kārayai|

asappurisasaṃsaggau, dukkhantau kaṭukudrayau||

164.

‘‘sacai  hi vīriyaṃ nāssa, kammaṃ kalyāṇapāpakaṃ|

na  bharai vaḍḍhakiṃ rājā, napi yantāni kārayai||

165.

‘‘yasmā ca vīriyaṃ atthi, kammaṃ kalyāṇapāpakaṃ|

tasmā yantāni kāraiti, rājā bharati vaḍḍhakiṃ||

166.

‘‘yadi vassasataṃ daivau, na vassai na himaṃ patai|

ucchij‍jaiyya ayaṃ laukau, vinassaiyya ayaṃ pajā||

167.

‘‘yasmā ca vassatī daivau, himañ‍cānuphusāyati|

tasmā sassāni pac‍canti, raṭṭhañ‍ca pālitai pal‍latai (sī॰ pī॰), paulayatai (syā॰) ciraṃ||

168.

‘‘gavaṃ cai taramānānaṃ, jimhaṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā tā jimhaṃ gacchanti, naittai jimhaṃ jimha (pī॰) gatai sati||

169.

‘‘aivamaiva aivamaivaṃ (pī॰) manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau cai adhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ dukhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti adhammikau||

170.

‘‘gavaṃ cai taramānānaṃ, ujuṃ gacchati puṅgavau|

sabbā gāvī ujuṃ yanti, naittai ujuṃ ujū (pī॰) gatai sati||

171.

‘‘aivamaiva manussaisu, yau hauti saiṭṭhasammatau|

sau sacai caiva (sī॰), caipi (ka॰) dhammaṃ carati, pagaiva itarā pajā|

sabbaṃ raṭṭhaṃ sukhaṃ saiti, rājā cai hauti dhammikau||

172.

‘‘mahārukkhassa phalinau, āmaṃ chindati yau phalaṃ|

rasañ‍cassa na jānāti, bījañ‍cassa vinassati||

173.

‘‘mahārukkhūpamaṃ  raṭṭhaṃ, adhammaina pasāsati|

rasañ‍cassa na jānāti, raṭṭhañ‍cassa vinassati||

174.

‘‘mahārukkhassa  phalinau, pak‍kaṃ chindati yau phalaṃ|

rasañ‍cassa vijānāti, bījañ‍cassa na nassati||

175.

‘‘mahārukkhūpamaṃ raṭṭhaṃ, dhammaina yau pasāsati|

rasañ‍cassa vijānāti, raṭṭhañ‍cassa na nassati||

176.

‘‘yau ca rājā janapadaṃ, adhammaina pasāsati|

sabbausadhīhi sau rājā, viruddhau hauti khattiyau||

177.

‘‘tathaiva naigamai hiṃsaṃ, yai yuttā kayavik‍kayai|

aujadānabalīkārai, sa kausaina virujjhati||

178.

‘‘pahāravarakhaittañ‍ñū, saṅgāmai katanissamai kataniyamai (ka॰)|

ussitai hiṃsayaṃ rājā, sa balaina virujjhati||

179.

‘‘tathaiva isayau hiṃsaṃ, sañ‍ñatai saṃyamai (syā॰ ka॰) brahmacāriyau brahmacārinau (sī॰)|

adhammacārī khattiyau, sau saggaina virujjhati||

180.

‘‘yau ca rājā adhammaṭṭhau, bhariyaṃ hanti adūsikaṃ|

luddaṃ pasavatai ṭhānaṃ pāpaṃ (sī॰), puttaihi ca virujjhati||

181.

‘‘dhammaṃ carai jānapadai, naigamaisu nigamaisu (sī॰) balaisu ca|

isayau ca na hiṃsaiyya, puttadārai samaṃ carai||

182.

‘‘sa tādisau bhūmipati, raṭṭhapālau akaudhanau|

sapattai  sāmantai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) sampakampaiti, indauva asurādhipau’’ti||

mahābaudhijātakaṃ tatiyaṃ|

paṇṇāsanipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

saniḷīnikamavhayanau paṭhamau, dutiyau pana saummadantivarau|

tatiyau pana baudhisirīvhayanau, kathitā pana tīṇi jinaina subhāti||


19. saṭṭhinipātau

529. sauṇakajātakaṃ (1)

1.

‘‘tassa  sutvā sataṃ dammi, sahassaṃ diṭṭha daṭṭhu (sī॰ pī॰) sauṇakaṃ|

kau mai sauṇakamakkhāti, sahāyaṃ paṃsukīḷitaṃ’’||

2.

‘‘athabravī māṇavakau, daharau pañ‍cacūḷakau|

mayhaṃ sutvā sataṃ daihi, sahassaṃ diṭṭha daṭṭhu (sī॰ pī॰) sauṇakaṃ|

ahaṃ tai sauṇakakkhissaṃ ahaṃ sauṇakamakkhissaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), ahaṃ tai sauṇakamakkhissaṃ (syā॰), sahāyaṃ paṃsukīḷitaṃ’’||

3.

‘‘katamasmiṃ katarasmiṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) sau janapadai, raṭṭhaisu nigamaisu ca|

kattha sauṇakamaddakkhi kattha tai sauṇakau diṭṭhau (sī॰ pī॰), taṃ mai akkhāhi pucchitau’’||

4.

‘‘tavaiva daiva vijitai, tavaivuyyānabhūmiyā|

ujuvaṃsā mahāsālā, nīlaubhāsā manauramā||

5.

‘‘tiṭṭhanti maighasamānā, rammā añ‍ñauñ‍ñanissitā|

taisaṃ mūlamhi mūlasmiṃ (sī॰ pī॰), mūlasmi (syā॰) sauṇakau, jhāyatī anupādanau anupādinau (syā॰), anupādānau (pī॰)|

upādānaisu laukaisu, ḍayhamānaisu nibbutau||

6.

‘‘tatau ca rājā pāyāsi, saināya caturaṅgiyā|

kārāpaitvā samaṃ maggaṃ, agamā yaina sauṇakau||

7.

‘‘uyyānabhūmiṃ gantvāna, vicarantau brahāvanai|

āsīnaṃ  sauṇakaṃ dakkhi, ḍayhamānaisu nibbutaṃ’’||

8.

‘‘kapaṇau vatayaṃ bhikkhu, muṇḍau saṅghāṭipārutau|

amātikau apitikau, rukkhamūlasmi jhāyati’’||

9.

‘‘imaṃ vākyaṃ nisāmaitvā, sauṇakau aitadabravi|

‘na rāja kapaṇau hauti, dhammaṃ kāyaina phassayaṃ phusayaṃ (ka॰)||

10.

‘yau  ca yaudha (sī॰ syā॰) dhammaṃ niraṃkatvā nirākatvā (?), adhammamanuvattati|

sa rāja kapaṇau hauti, pāpau pāpaparāyanau’’’||

11.

‘‘‘arindamauti  mai nāmaṃ, kāsirājāti maṃ vidū|

kac‍ci bhautau sukhassaiyyā sukhā saiyyā (pī॰), sukhasaiyyau (ka॰), idha pattassa sauṇaka’’’||

12.

‘‘sadāpi bhadramadhanassa, anāgārassa bhikkhunau|

na taisaṃ kauṭṭhai aupainti, na kumbhiṃ na khaḷaupiyaṃ na kumbhai na kaḷaupiyā (syā॰ pī॰)|

paraniṭṭhitamaisānā, taina yāpainti subbatā||

13.

‘‘dutiyampi bhadramadhanassa, anāgārassa bhikkhunau|

anavaj‍japiṇḍau anavaj‍jau piṇḍā (pī॰) bhauttabbau, na ca kaucūparaudhati||

14.

‘‘tatiyampi bhadramadhanassa, anāgārassa bhikkhunau|

nibbutau piṇḍau bhauttabbau, na ca kaucūparaudhati||

15.

‘‘catutthampi catutthaṃ (pī॰) bhadramadhanassa, anāgārassa bhikkhunau|

muttassa raṭṭhai caratau, saṅgau yassa na vij‍jati||

16.

‘‘pañ‍camampi pañ‍camaṃ (pī॰) bhadramadhanassa, anāgārassa bhikkhunau|

nagaramhi ḍayhamānamhi, nāssa kiñ‍ci aḍayhatha||

17.

‘‘chaṭṭhampi  chaṭṭhaṃ (pī॰) bhadramadhanassa, anāgārassa bhikkhunau|

raṭṭhai vilumpamānamhi viluppamānamhi (ka॰), nāssa kiñ‍ci ahīratha ahāratha (sī॰ syā॰)||

18.

‘‘sattamampi sattamaṃ (pī॰) bhadramadhanassa, anāgārassa bhikkhunau|

cauraihi rakkhitaṃ maggaṃ, yai cañ‍ñai paripanthikā|

pattacīvaramādāya, sautthiṃ gacchati subbatau||

19.

‘‘aṭṭhamampi aṭṭhamaṃ (pī॰) bhadramadhanassa, anāgārassa bhikkhunau|

yaṃ yaṃ disaṃ pak‍kamati, anapaikkhauva gacchati’’||

20.

‘‘bahūpi bhadrā bahūni samaṇabhadrāni (sī॰), bahūpi bhadrakā aitai (pī॰) aitaisaṃ, yau tvaṃ bhikkhu pasaṃsasi|

ahañ‍ca giddhau kāmaisu, kathaṃ kāhāmi sauṇaka||

21.

‘‘piyā  mai mānusā kāmā, athau dibyāpi mai piyā|

atha kaina nu vaṇṇaina, ubhau laukai labhāmasai’’||

22.

‘‘kāmai giddhā kāmaisu giddhā (sī॰ pī॰) kāmaratā, kāmaisu adhimucchitā|

narā pāpāni katvāna, upapaj‍janti duggatiṃ||

23.

‘‘yai ca kāmai pahantvāna pahatvāna (sī॰ pī॰), nikkhantā akutaubhayā|

aikaudibhāvādhigatā, na tai gacchanti duggatiṃ||

24.

‘‘upamaṃ tai karissāmi, taṃ suṇauhi arindama|

upamāya midhaikac‍cai pidhaikac‍cai (sī॰ pī॰), atthaṃ jānanti paṇḍitā||

25.

‘‘gaṅgāya  kuṇapaṃ disvā, vuyhamānaṃ mahaṇṇavai|

vāyasau samacintaisi, appapañ‍ñau acaitasau||

26.

‘‘‘yānañ‍ca  vatidaṃ laddhaṃ, bhakkhau cāyaṃ anappakau’|

tattha rattiṃ tattha divā, tatthaiva niratau manau||

27.

‘‘khādaṃ nāgassa maṃsāni, pivaṃ bhāgīrathaudakaṃ bhāgirasaudakaṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰ ka॰)|

sampassaṃ vanacaityāni, na palaittha palaitvā (ka॰) vihaṅgamau||

28.

‘‘tañ‍ca taṃva (pī॰) autaraṇī gaṅgā, pamattaṃ kuṇapai rataṃ|

samuddaṃ ajjhagāhāsi ajjhagāhayi (pī॰), agatī yattha pakkhinaṃ||

29.

‘‘sau ca bhakkhaparikkhīṇau, udapatvā uppatitvā (sī॰ syā॰), udāpatvā (pī॰) vihaṅgamau||

na pacchatau na puratau, nuttaraṃ naupi dakkhiṇaṃ||

30.

‘‘dīpaṃ sau najjhagāgañchi na ajjhagañchi (sī॰), na ajjhagacchi (pī॰), agatī yattha pakkhinaṃ|

sau ca tatthaiva pāpattha, yathā dubbalakau tathā||

31.

‘‘tañ‍ca sāmuddikā macchā, kumbhīlā makarā susū|

pasayhakārā khādiṃsu, phandamānaṃ vipakkhakaṃ vipakkhinaṃ (sī॰ pī॰), vipakkhikaṃ (syā॰)||

32.

‘‘aivamaiva  tuvaṃ rāja, yai cañ‍ñai kāmabhauginau|

giddhā cai na vamissanti, kākapañ‍ñāva kākapañ‍ñāya (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) tai vidū||

33.

‘‘aisā tai upamā rāja, atthasandassanī katā|

tvañ‍ca pañ‍ñāyasai taina, yadi kāhasi vā na vā||

34.

‘‘aikavācampi dvivācaṃ, bhaṇaiyya anukampakau|

tatuttariṃ na bhāsaiyya, dāsauvayyassa dāsau ayyassa (sī॰), dāsau ayirassa (pī॰) santikai’’||

35.

‘‘idaṃ vatvāna pak‍kāmi, sauṇakau amitabuddhimā sauṇakau’mitabuddhimā (?)|

vaihāsai antalikkhasmiṃ, anusāsitvāna khattiyaṃ’’||

36.

‘‘kau  numai rājakattārau, suddā vaiyyattamāgatā sūtā vaiyyattimāgatā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰)|

raj‍jaṃ niyyādayissāmi, nāhaṃ raj‍jaina matthikau||

37.

‘‘aj‍jaiva pabbajissāmi, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

māhaṃ kākauva dummaidhau, kāmānaṃ vasamanvagaṃ’’ vasaman‍nagā (pī॰)||

38.

‘‘atthi tai daharau puttau, dīghāvu raṭṭhavaḍḍhanau|

taṃ raj‍jai abhisiñ‍cassu, sau nau rājā bhavissati’’||

39.

‘‘khippaṃ kumāramānaitha, dīghāvuṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanaṃ|

taṃ raj‍jai abhisiñ‍cissaṃ, sau vau rājā bhavissati’’||

40.

‘‘tatau  kumāramānaisuṃ, dīghāvuṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanaṃ|

taṃ disvā ālapī rājā, aikaputtaṃ manauramaṃ||

41.

‘‘saṭṭhi gāmasahassāni, paripuṇṇāni sabbasau|

tai putta paṭipaj‍jassu, raj‍jaṃ niyyādayāmi tai||

42.

‘‘aj‍jaiva pabbajissāmi, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

māhaṃ kākauva dummaidhau, kāmānaṃ vasamanvagaṃ vasaman‍nagā (pī॰)||

43.

‘‘saṭṭhi nāgasahassāni, sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā|

suvaṇṇakacchā mātaṅgā, haimakappanavāsasā||

44.

‘‘ārūḷhā  gāmaṇīyaihi, taumaraṅkusapāṇibhi|

tai putta paṭipaj‍jassu, raj‍jaṃ niyyādayāmi tai||

45.

‘‘aj‍jaiva pabbajissāmi, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

māhaṃ kākauva dummaidhau, kāmānaṃ vasamanvagaṃ||

46.

‘‘saṭṭhi  assasahassāni, sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā|

ājānīyāva jātiyā, sindhavā sīghavāhinau||

47.

‘‘ārūḷhā gāmaṇīyaihi, il‍liyācāpadhāribhi indiyācāpadhāribhi (ka॰)|

tai putta paṭipaj‍jassu, raj‍jaṃ niyyādayāmi tai||

48.

‘‘aj‍jaiva pabbajissāmi, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

māhaṃ kākauva dummaidhau, kāmānaṃ vasamanvagaṃ||

49.

‘‘saṭṭhi rathasahassāni, san‍naddhā ussitaddhajā|

dīpā athaupi vaiyyagghā, sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā||

50.

‘‘ārūḷhā gāmaṇīyaihi, cāpahatthaihi vammibhi|

tai putta paṭipaj‍jassu, raj‍jaṃ niyyādayāmi tai||

51.

‘‘aj‍jaiva pabbajissāmi, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

māhaṃ kākauva dummaidhau, kāmānaṃ vasamanvagaṃ||

52.

‘‘saṭṭhi dhainusahassāni, rauhañ‍ñā puṅgavūsabhā|

tā putta paṭipaj‍jassu, raj‍jaṃ niyyādayāmi tai||

53.

‘‘aj‍jaiva pabbajissāmi, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

māhaṃ kākauva dummaidhau, kāmānaṃ vasamanvagaṃ||

54.

‘‘sauḷasitthisahassāni, sabbālaṅkārabhūsitā|

vicitravatthābharaṇā, āmuttamaṇikuṇḍalā|

tā putta paṭipaj‍jassu, raj‍jaṃ niyyādayāmi tai||

55.

‘‘aj‍jaiva pabbajissāmi, kau jañ‍ñā maraṇaṃ suvai|

māhaṃ  kākauva dummaidhau, kāmānaṃ vasamanvagaṃ’’||

56.

‘‘daharassaiva  mai tāta, mātā matāti mai sutaṃ|

tayā vinā ahaṃ tāta, jīvitumpi na ussahai||

57.

‘‘yathā ārañ‍ñakaṃ nāgaṃ, pautau anvaiti pacchatau|

jaissantaṃ giriduggaisu, samaisu visamaisu ca||

58.

‘‘aivaṃ taṃ anugacchāmi, puttamādāya pattamādāya (pī॰) pacchatau|

subharau tai bhavissāmi, na tai haissāmi dubbharau’’||

59.

‘‘yathā sāmuddikaṃ nāvaṃ, vāṇijānaṃ dhanaisinaṃ|

vauhārau tattha gaṇhaiyya, vāṇijā byasanī byasanaṃ (ka॰) siyā||

60.

‘‘aivamaivāyaṃ puttakali puttaka (syā॰), antarāyakarau mama mamaṃ (pī॰)|

imaṃ kumāraṃ pāpaitha, pāsādaṃ rativaḍḍhanaṃ||

61.

‘‘tattha kambusahatthāyau, yathā sak‍kaṃva accharā|

tā naṃ tattha ramaissanti ramissanti (syā॰ ka॰), tāhi caisau maisau (pī॰) ramissati||

62.

‘‘tatau kumāraṃ pāpaisuṃ, pāsādaṃ rativaḍḍhanaṃ|

taṃ disvā avacuṃ kañ‍ñā, dīghāvuṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanaṃ||

63.

‘‘daivatā nusi gandhabbau, adu ādu (sī॰ pī॰) sak‍kau purindadau|

kau vā tvaṃ kassa vā puttau, kathaṃ jānaimu taṃ mayaṃ’’||

64.

‘‘namhi daivau na gandhabbau, nāpi namhi (ka॰) sak‍kau purindadau|

kāsirañ‍ñau ahaṃ puttau, dīghāvu raṭṭhavaḍḍhanau|

mamaṃ mama (pī॰) bharatha bhaddaṃ vau bhaddantai (ka॰), ahaṃ bhattā bhavāmi vau’’||

65.

‘‘taṃ  tattha avacuṃ kañ‍ñā, dīghāvuṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanaṃ|

‘kuhiṃ rājā anuppattau, itau rājā kuhiṃ gatau’’’||

66.

‘‘paṅkaṃ rājā atik‍kantau, thalai rājā patiṭṭhitau|

akaṇṭakaṃ agahanaṃ, paṭipan‍nau mahāpathaṃ||

67.

‘‘ahañ‍ca paṭipan‍nausmi, maggaṃ duggatigāminaṃ|

sakaṇṭakaṃ sagahanaṃ, yaina gacchanti duggatiṃ’’||

68.

‘‘tassa  tai svāgataṃ rāja, sīhassaiva giribbajaṃ|

anusāsa mahārāja, tvaṃ nau sabbāsamissarau’’ti||

sauṇakajātakaṃ paṭhamaṃ|

530. saṃkic‍cajātakaṃ (2)

69.

‘‘disvā nisin‍naṃ rājānaṃ, brahmadattaṃ rathaisabhaṃ|

athassa paṭivaidaisi, yassāsi anukampakau||

70.

‘‘saṃkic‍cāyaṃ anuppattau, isīnaṃ sādhusammatau|

taramānarūpau niyyāhi, khippaṃ passa mahaisinaṃ||

71.

‘‘tatau ca rājā taramānau, yuttamāruyha sandanaṃ|

mittāmac‍caparibyūḷhau paribbūḷhau (sī॰ pī॰), agamāsi rathaisabhau||

72.

‘‘nikkhippa pañ‍ca kakudhāni, kāsīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanau|

vāḷabījani vā ḷavījanī (sī॰ pī॰) muṇhīsaṃ, khaggaṃ chattañ‍cupāhanaṃ|

73.

‘‘auruyha rājā yānamhā, ṭhapayitvā paṭicchadaṃ|

āsīnaṃ  dāyapassasmiṃ, saṃkic‍camupasaṅkami||

74.

‘‘upasaṅkamitvā sau rājā, sammaudi isinā saha|

taṃ kathaṃ vītisāraitvā, aikamantaṃ upāvisi||

75.

‘‘aikamantaṃ nisin‍nauva, atha kālaṃ amañ‍ñatha|

tatau pāpāni kammāni, pucchituṃ paṭipaj‍jatha||

76.

‘‘isiṃ pucchāma pucchāmi (sī॰ pī॰) saṃkic‍caṃ, isīnaṃ sādhusammataṃ|

āsīnaṃ dāyapassasmiṃ, isisaṅghapurakkhataṃ purakkhitaṃ (ka॰)||

77.

‘‘kaṃ gatiṃ paic‍ca gacchanti, narā dhammāticārinau|

aticiṇṇau mayā dhammau, taṃ mai akkhāhi pucchitau||

78.

‘‘isī avaca saṃkic‍cau, kāsīnaṃ raṭṭhavaḍḍhanaṃ|

āsīnaṃ dāyapassasmiṃ, mahārāja suṇauhi mai||

79.

‘‘uppathaina  vajantassa, yau maggamanusāsati|

tassa cai vacanaṃ kayirā, nāssa maggaiyya kaṇṭakau||

80.

‘‘adhammaṃ  paṭipan‍nassa, yau dhammamanusāsati|

tassa cai vacanaṃ kayirā, na sau gacchaiyya duggatiṃ||

81.

‘‘dhammau pathau mahārāja, adhammau pana uppathau|

adhammau nirayaṃ naiti, dhammau pāpaiti suggatiṃ||

82.

‘‘adhammacārinau rāja, narā visamajīvinau|

yaṃ gatiṃ paic‍ca gacchanti, nirayai tai suṇauhi mai||

83.

‘‘sañ‍jīvau kāḷasuttau ca, saṅghātau saṅkhāṭau (syā॰ ka॰) dvai ca rauruvā|

athāparau  mahāvīci, tāpanau tapanau (sī॰ pī॰) ca patāpanau||

84.

‘‘ic‍caitai aṭṭha nirayā, akkhātā duratik‍kamā|

ākiṇṇā luddakammaihi, pac‍caikā sauḷasussadā||

85.

‘‘kadariyatāpanā kadariyatapanā (sī॰ pī॰) ghaurā, ac‍cimantau ac‍cimantā (pī॰) mahabbhayā|

laumahaṃsanarūpā ca, bhaismā paṭibhayā dukhā||

86.

‘‘catuk‍kaṇṇā catudvārā, vibhattā bhāgasau mitā|

ayaupākārapariyantā, ayasā paṭikuj‍jitā||

87.

‘‘taisaṃ ayaumayā bhūmi, jalitā taijasā yutā|

samantā yaujanasataṃ, phuṭā pharitvā (a॰ ni॰ 3.36; pai॰ va॰ 71) tiṭṭhanti sabbadā||

88.

‘‘aitai patanti nirayai, uddhaṃpādā avaṃsirā|

isīnaṃ ativattārau, sañ‍ñatānaṃ tapassinaṃ||

89.

‘‘tai bhūnahunau pac‍canti, macchā bilakatā yathā|

saṃvaccharai asaṅkhaiyyai, narā kibbisakārinau||

90.

‘‘ḍayhamānaina gattaina, nic‍caṃ santarabāhiraṃ|

nirayā nādhigacchanti, dvāraṃ nikkhamanaisinau||

91.

‘‘puratthimaina  dhāvanti, tatau dhāvanti pacchatau|

uttarainapi dhāvanti, tatau dhāvanti dakkhiṇaṃ|

yaṃ yañhi dvāraṃ gacchanti, taṃ tadaiva pidhīyarai pithiyyati (sī॰), pithiyyarai (syā॰), pithīyarai (pī॰)||

92.

‘‘bahūni vassasahassāni, janā nirayagāminau|

bāhā paggayha kandanti, patvā dukkhaṃ anappakaṃ||

93.

‘‘āsīvisaṃva  kupitaṃ, taijassiṃ duratik‍kamaṃ|

na sādhurūpai āsīdai, sañ‍ñatānaṃ tapassinaṃ||

94.

‘‘atikāyau  mahissāsau, aj‍junau kaikakādhipau|

sahassabāhu ucchin‍nau, isimāsaj‍ja gautamaṃ||

95.

‘‘arajaṃ rajasā vacchaṃ, kisaṃ avakiriya daṇḍakī|

tālauva mūlatau samūlau (ka॰) chin‍nau, sa rājā vibhavaṅgatau||

96.

‘‘upahac‍ca manaṃ majjhau maijjhau (ka॰), mātaṅgasmiṃ yasassinai|

sapārisaj‍jau ucchin‍nau, majjhārañ‍ñaṃ tadā ahu||

97.

‘‘kaṇhadīpāyanāsaj‍ja, isiṃ andhakavaiṇḍayau vaiṇhuyau (sī॰ pī॰), piṇhayau (?)|

añ‍ñauñ‍ñaṃ añ‍ñamañ‍ñaṃ (sī॰ pī॰) musalā musalai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) hantvā, sampattā yamasādhanaṃ yamasādanaṃ (pī॰)||

98.

‘‘athāyaṃ isinā sattau, antalikkhacarau purai|

pāvaikkhi pathaviṃ paṭhaviṃ (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) caic‍cau, hīnattau pattapariyāyaṃ||

99.

‘‘tasmā hi chandāgamanaṃ, nappasaṃsanti paṇḍitā|

aduṭṭhacittau bhāsaiyya, giraṃ sac‍cūpasaṃhitaṃ||

100.

‘‘manasā cai paduṭṭhaina, yau narau paikkhatai muniṃ|

vij‍jācaraṇasampan‍naṃ, gantā sau nirayaṃ adhau||

101.

‘‘yai vuḍḍhai vaddhai (ka॰) paribhāsanti, pharusūpak‍kamā janā|

anapac‍cā adāyādā, tālavatthu tālavatthū (syā॰), tālāvatthu (pī॰) bhavanti tai||

102.

‘‘yau  ca pabbajitaṃ hanti, katakic‍caṃ mahaisinaṃ|

sa kāḷasuttai nirayai, cirarattāya pac‍cati||

103.

‘‘yau  ca rājā adhammaṭṭhau, raṭṭhaviddhaṃsanau magau cutau (sī॰)|

tāpayitvā janapadaṃ, tāpanai paic‍ca pac‍cati||

104.

‘‘sau ca vassasahassāni vassasahassānaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), sataṃ dibbāni pac‍cati|

ac‍cisaṅghaparaitau sau, dukkhaṃ vaidaiti vaidanaṃ||

105.

‘‘tassa aggisikhā kāyā, niccharanti pabhassarā|

taijaubhakkhassa gattāni, laumaihi ca laumaggaihi ca (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) nakhaihi ca||

106.

‘‘ḍayhamānaina gattaina, nic‍caṃ santarabāhiraṃ|

dukkhābhitun‍nau nadati, nāgau tuttaṭṭitau tuttadditau (sī॰) yathā||

107.

‘‘yau laubhā pitaraṃ hanti, dausā vā purisādhamau|

sa kāḷasuttai nirayai, cirarattāya pac‍cati||

108.

‘‘sa  tādisau pac‍cati lauhakumbhiyaṃ, pak‍kañ‍ca sattīhi hananti nittacaṃ|

andhaṃ karitvā muttakarīsabhakkhaṃ, khārai nimuj‍janti tathāvidhaṃ naraṃ||

109.

‘‘tattaṃ pak‍kuthitamayauguḷañ‍ca pak‍kudhitamayauguḷañ‍ca (ka॰), dīghai ca phālai cirarattatāpitai|

vikkhambhamādāya vibandha vibaddha (sī॰), vibhaj‍ja (syā॰ pī॰) raj‍jubhi, vivaṭai mukhai sampavisanti saṃcavanti (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) rakkhasā||

110.

‘‘sāmā ca sauṇā sabalā ca gijjhā, kākauḷasaṅghā ca dijā ayaumukhā|

saṅgamma  khādanti vipphandamānaṃ, jivhaṃ vibhaj‍ja vighāsaṃ salauhitaṃ||

111.

‘‘taṃ  daḍḍhatālaṃ paribhin‍nagattaṃ, nippauthayantā anuvicaranti rakkhasā|

ratī hi naisaṃ dukhinau panītarai, aitādisasmiṃ nirayai vasanti|

yai kaici laukai idha paittighātinau||

112.

‘‘puttau ca mātaraṃ hantvā, itau gantvā yamakkhayaṃ|

bhusamāpaj‍jatai dukkhaṃ, attakammaphalūpagau||

113.

‘‘amanussā atibalā, hantāraṃ janayantiyā|

ayaumayaihi vāḷaihi phālaihi (pī॰), pīḷayanti punappunaṃ||

114.

‘‘tamassavaṃ taṃ passavaṃ (sī॰ syā॰), taṃ passutaṃ (pī॰) sakā gattā, ruhiraṃ rudhiraṃ (sī॰ syā॰) attasambhavaṃ|

tambalauhavilīnaṃva, tattaṃ pāyainti mattighaṃ mattiyaṃ (sī॰)||

115.

‘‘jigucchaṃ kuṇapaṃ pūtiṃ, duggandhaṃ gūthakaddamaṃ|

pubbalauhitasaṅkāsaṃ, rahadamaugayha rahadauggayha (ka॰) tiṭṭhati||

116.

‘‘tamainaṃ kimayau tattha, atikāyā ayaumukhā|

chaviṃ bhaitvāna chaitvāna (sī॰ pī॰) khādanti, saṃgiddhā pagiddhā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) maṃsalauhitai||

117.

‘‘sau ca taṃ nirayaṃ pattau, nimuggau satapaurisaṃ|

pūtikaṃ kuṇapaṃ vāti, samantā satayaujanaṃ||

118.

‘‘cakkhumāpi hi cakkhūhi, taina gandhaina jīyati|

aitādisaṃ  brahmadatta, mātughau labhatai dukhaṃ||

119.

‘‘khuradhāramanuk‍kamma, tikkhaṃ durabhisambhavaṃ|

patanti gabbhapātiyau gabbhapātiniyau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), duggaṃ vaitaraṇiṃ vaittaraṇiṃ (syā॰ ka॰) nadiṃ||

120.

‘‘ayaumayā simbaliyau, sauḷasaṅgulakaṇṭakā|

ubhatau abhilambanti, duggaṃ vaitaraṇiṃ vaittaraṇiṃ (syā॰ ka॰) nadiṃ||

121.

‘‘tai  ac‍cimantau tiṭṭhanti, aggikkhandhāva ārakā|

ādittā jātavaidaina, uddhaṃ yaujanamuggatā||

122.

‘‘aitai  vajanti sajanti (sī॰ pī॰), paj‍janti (syā॰) nirayai, tattai tikhiṇakaṇṭakai|

nāriyau ca aticārā aticāriniyau (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), narā ca paradāragū||

123.

‘‘tai patanti adhaukkhandhā, vivattā vihatā puthū|

sayanti vinividdhaṅgā, dīghaṃ jagganti sabbadā saṃvariṃ (sī॰ pī॰)||

124.

‘‘tatau ratyā vivasānai vivasanai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰), mahatiṃ pabbatūpamaṃ|

lauhakumbhiṃ pavaj‍janti, tattaṃ aggisamūdakaṃ||

125.

‘‘aivaṃ divā ca rattau ca, dussīlā mauhapārutā|

anubhaunti sakaṃ kammaṃ, pubbai duk‍kaṭamattanau||

126.

‘‘yā ca bhariyā dhanak‍kītā, sāmikaṃ atimañ‍ñati|

sassuṃ vā sasuraṃ vāpi, jaiṭṭhaṃ vāpi nanandaraṃ nanandanaṃ (syā॰ ka॰)||

127.

‘‘tassā vaṅkaina jivhaggaṃ, nibbahanti sabandhanaṃ|

sa byāmamattaṃ kiminaṃ, jivhaṃ passati attani attanau (sī॰ syā॰)|

viñ‍ñāpaituṃ  na sak‍kauti, tāpanai paic‍ca pac‍cati||

128.

‘‘aurabbhikā sūkarikā, macchikā migabandhakā|

caurā gaughātakā luddā, avaṇṇai vaṇṇakārakā||

129.

‘‘sattīhi lauhakūṭaihi, naittiṃsaihi usūhi ca|

hañ‍ñamānā khāranadiṃ, papatanti sampatanti (ka॰) avaṃsirā||

130.

‘‘sāyaṃ pātau kūṭakārī, ayaukūṭaihi hañ‍ñati|

tatau vantaṃ durattānaṃ, paraisaṃ bhuñ‍jarai bhuñ‍jatai (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) sadā||

131.

‘‘dhaṅkā bhairaṇḍakā bhaidaṇḍakā (ka॰) gijjhā, kākauḷā ca ayaumukhā|

vipphandamānaṃ khādanti, naraṃ kibbisakārakaṃ kibbisakārinaṃ (pī॰)||

132.

‘‘yai migaina migaṃ hanti, pakkhiṃ vā pana pakkhinā|

asantau rajasā chan‍nā, gantā gatā (ka॰) tai nirayussadaṃ nirayaṃ adhau (pī॰)||

133.

‘‘santau  ca santauva (syā॰) uddhaṃ gacchanti, suciṇṇainidha kammunā|

suciṇṇassa phalaṃ passa, saindā sahindā (sī॰) daivā sabrahmakā||

134.

‘‘taṃ  taṃ brūmi mahārāja, dhammaṃ raṭṭhapatī cara|

tathā tathā tathā (sī॰ syā॰ pī॰) rāja carāhi dhammaṃ, yathā taṃ suciṇṇaṃ nānutappaiyya pacchā’’ti||

saṃkic‍cajātakaṃ dutiyaṃ|

saṭṭhinipātaṃ niṭṭhitaṃ|


tassuddānaṃ –

atha saṭṭhinipātamhi, suṇātha mama bhāsitaṃ|

jātakasavhayanau  pavarau, sauṇakaarindamasavhayanau|

tathā vuttarathaisabhakic‍cavarauti||


20. sattatinipātau

531. kusajātakaṃ (1)

1.

‘‘idaṃ  tai raṭṭhaṃ sadhanaṃ sayauggaṃ, sakāyuraṃ sabbakāmūpapan‍naṃ|

idaṃ tai raj‍jaṃ raṭṭhaṃ (ka॰) anusāsa amma, gacchāmahaṃ yattha piyā pabhāvatī’’||

2.

‘‘anuj‍jubhūtaina haraṃ mahantaṃ, divā ca rattau ca nisīthakālai nisīda kālai (ka॰)|

paṭigaccha tvaṃ khippaṃ kusāvatiṃ kusa kusāvatiṃ (syā॰ ka॰), nicchāmi dubbaṇṇamahaṃ vasantaṃ’’||

3.

‘‘nāhaṃ gamissāmi itau kusāvatiṃ, pabhāvatī vaṇṇapalaubhitau tava|

ramāmi maddassa nikaitarammai, hitvāna raṭṭhaṃ tava dassanai ratau||

4.

‘‘pabhāvatī vaṇṇapalaubhitau tava, sammūḷharūpau vicarāmi maidiniṃ maidaniṃ (syā॰ ka॰)|
